Conclusion: 对于具有有限加权矩的局域源，光滑running维持前导k³红外行为（仅含对数修正）。由于PBH质量满足M∝k⁻²，高波数偏好加权在窄支撑解释下有条件地偏好较低PBH质量。峰值位置、谱曲率、肩部及紫外斜率可作为PTA、LISA和第三代观测的诊断量，为不可达尺度上的原初相互作用提供有限阶模板。

Abstract: Scalar-induced gravitational waves (SIGWs) probe primordial curvature perturbations beyond cosmic microwave background scales, while primordial black holes (PBHs) probe the rare tail of the same statistics. We ask whether SIGW morphology can reveal scale-dependent primordial non-Gaussianity. We adopt the internal-leg separable kernel, $\mathcal F_{\rm NL}(\mathbf{k};\mathbf q,\mathbf{k}-\mathbf q)=\bar f_{\rm NL}f(q)f(\lVert\mathbf{k}-\mathbf q\rVert)$, and compute Gaussian, reducible, and connected contributions through $\mathcal O(\bar f_{\rm NL}^{4})$ in the second-order tensor-source approximation. We compare persistent power-law running with a UV-matched tanh weight that saturates at high scalar momentum. Across narrow, finite-width, asymmetric, and multi-slope scalar spectra, running produces more than amplitude renormalization. Power-law running shifts the scalar support sampled by non-Gaussian convolutions, generating peak displacement, asymmetric shoulders, and persistent ultraviolet deformations. The tanh template instead produces a transition-localized modification and approaches a momentum-independent ultraviolet plateau. Analytical estimates of the perturbative hierarchy, weighted slopes, log-normal saddle displacement, peak shifts, and infrared scaling explain the trends and the enhanced sensitivity of the $\mathcal O(\bar f_{\rm NL}^{4})$ sector. For localized sources with finite weighted moments, smooth running preserves the leading $k^3$ infrared behavior up to logarithmic corrections. Since the PBH mass scales as $M\propto k^{-2}$, preferential high-wavenumber weighting conditionally favors lower PBH masses in a narrow-support interpretation. Peak position, spectral curvature, shoulders, and ultraviolet slopes are diagnostics for PTAs, LISA, and third-generation observations, providing finite-order templates for primordial interactions on otherwise inaccessible scales.

</details>

### [93] [Photon and gluon gravitational form factors of proton in QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23788)

TL;DR: 本文在量子色动力学求和规则（QCD sum rules）框架内计算了质子的光子与胶子引力形状因子（gravitational form factors），并使用多极参数化（multipole parametrization）外推到零动量转移，进而提取光子与胶子扇区的质量和标量半径。结果表明胶子质量半径小于光子质量半径，暗示质子的胶子能量分布更加局域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能量-动量张量（energy-momentum tensor）是理解质子内部引力结构的关键工具，但不同量子色动力学（QCD）组分的引力形状因子尚未被充分计算。为获取各组分对质子引力性质的独立贡献，需要开展非微扰层面的系统研究。

Method: 在QCD求和规则框架下，分别采用光子和胶子组分的QCD能量-动量张量，推导四个独立引力形状因子对应的求和规则；在方法有效区域内分析形状因子的动量转移依赖，并通过多极参数化外推至零动量转移，最终提取各扇区的质量半径与标量半径。

Result: 计算结果给出光子与胶子引力形状因子的参数化形式，并提取了相应的质量半径与标量半径。结果显示胶子质量半径小于光子质量半径，表明质子的胶子能量分布较光子更为局域化。

Conclusion: 这项工作为质子引力结构提供了新的非微扰信息，并为未来的格点量子色动力学（lattice QCD）计算和唯象学研究提供了互补的理论参考结果。

Abstract: The energy-momentum tensor provides a unique insight into the internal gravitational structure of the proton by revealing how energy, momentum, and mechanical properties are distributed among its different QCD components. The gravitational form factors associated with different components of the QCD energy-momentum tensor allow us to investigate the individual contributions of these sectors to the gravitational properties of the proton. In this work, we calculate the photon and gluon gravitational form factors of the proton within the framework of QCD sum rules. Using the photon and gluon components of the QCD energy-momentum tensor, we derive the corresponding sum rules for four independent gravitational form factors. Their momentum transfer dependence is analyzed within the validity region of the method and extrapolated to zero momentum transfer using a multipole parametrization. The resulting parametrizations of the form factors are used to extract the mass and scalar radii associated with the photon and gluon sectors. Our results indicate that the gluon mass radius is smaller than the photon mass radius, suggesting a more localized gluonic energy distribution inside the proton. These findings provide new nonperturbative information on the gravitational structure of the proton and offer complementary theoretical results for future lattice QCD calculations and phenomenological studies.

</details>


### [94] [Probing Dirac Dark Matter in Composite Higgs Models with Xenon and Argon targets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24791)
*M. G. Belyakova,R. Nevzorov*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在复合希格斯模型（Composite Higgs Models, CHMs）框架下，研究带非零磁偶极矩（magnetic dipole moment）的最轻狄拉克复合粒子（lightest Dirac composite particle, LDCP）与核子及氙（Xe）、氩（Ar）原子核的弹性散射，发现其磁矩可在低反冲能量处显著增强直接探测（direct detection, DD）实验中的微分事件率，并在ξ≥0.1条件下识别了可能可观测的参数空间区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接探测实验的近期进展推动了暗物质（dark matter, DM）与核子及原子核相互作用的研究。作者希望探究在CHMs框架下，具有非零磁偶极矩的LDCP作为暗物质候选粒子时，其电磁性质能否在现有直接探测实验中产生可观测的信号增强，并与其他不具备类似电磁性质的暗物质粒子类型相区分。

Method: 作者在CHMs框架下，考虑LDCP与核子的弹性散射以及与氙和氩原子核的弹性散射，其中LDCP的磁矩、质量及其与希格斯二重态（Higgs doublet）的耦合被一个近似的U(1)对称性所压低。作者分析LDCP非零磁偶极矩对低反冲能量下微分事件率的贡献，并假设暗物质剩余丰度占比ξ≥0.1，扫描参数空间以识别该增强效应可能被观测的区域。

Result: 研究表明，具有非零磁偶极矩的LDCP可在直接探测实验的低反冲能区对微分事件率产生显著增强。在ξ≥0.1的假设下，作者识别出参数空间中该增强效应可能被观测的特定区域，并给出可用于区分具磁矩的暗物质费米子与不具备类似电磁性质的暗物质粒子的若干可观测量。

Conclusion: 具有非零磁矩的LDCP是直接探测实验中值得关注的暗物质候选粒子，其磁偶极矩导致的低能区事件率增强可能在现有实验灵敏度范围内被观测到，同时相关可观测量可为区分不同电磁性质的暗物质模型提供判别依据。

Abstract: Recent advancements in direct detection (DD) experiments stimulate the investigation of the interactions of dark matter (DM) with nucleons and nuclei. In the framework of Composite Higgs Models (CHMs) the lightest Dirac composite particle (LDCP) can be stable composing a significant fraction ξof the observed DM relic abundance. We consider the elastic scattering of the LDCP on nucleons as well as on xenon (Xe) and argon (Ar) nuclei within the CHMs in which the LDCP magnetic moment, its mass and its coupling to the Higgs doublet are suppressed by an approximate U(1) symmetry. The LDCP with non-zero magnetic dipole moment can result in a substantial enhancement of the differential event rate in the DD experiments at low recoil energies of nuclei. Assuming ξ\ge 0.1, we identify the region of the parameter space where such enhancement may be potentially observable. In addition we specify some observables that can be useful in discriminating between the DM fermions with non-zero magnetic moment and other types of dark matter particles which don't have similar electromagnetic properties.

</details>


### [95] [Symbolic Classification-Enabled LHC Limits Online BSM Global Fits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22330)
*Shehu AbdusSalam*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出利用符号回归（symbolic regression）技术将大型强子对撞机（LHC）的限制条件在线（online）纳入超越标准模型（Beyond the Standard Model, BSM）物理的全局拟合（global fit）中，解决以往计算成本过高的问题。作者基于ATLAS对电弱产生（electroweakino production）搜索的数据集，推导出能够分类phenomenological Minimal Supersymmetric Standard Model（pMSSM）参数空间的数学表达式，并将其用于包含LHC Run-2限制的pMSSM全局拟合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全局拟合中理论与实践之间存在双向反馈：理论模型指导实验搜索，实验结果约束理论框架。然而将LHC的限制直接纳入全局拟合的参数扫描过程在计算上代价高昂，每个参数点的计算时间往往超出全局拟合框架可接受的范围，因此需要一种更高效的方法。

Method: 利用符号回归（symbolic regression）技术从ATLAS针对电弱产生（electroweakino production）搜索的限制数据集中推导出一个数学表达式，该表达式能够将phenomenological Minimal Supersymmetric Standard Model（pMSSM）参数空间分类为允许或排除，随后将其在线（online）纳入pMSSM的全局拟合中以包含LHC Run-2限制。

Result: 成功展示了可以将LHC限制条件在线（online）整合进全局拟合框架，通过基于符号回归（symbolic regression）推导的近似表达式实现对phenomenological Minimal Supersymmetric Standard Model（pMSSM）参数空间的高效分类，并完成了包含LHC Run-2限制的pMSSM全局拟合。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，借助符号回归（symbolic regression）推导的近似手段，可以有效克服将LHC限制在线（online）纳入全局拟合的计算瓶颈，为超越标准模型（Beyond the Standard Model）全局拟合中理论与实验反馈循环的实际操作提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Global fits of Beyond the Standard Model (BSM) physics often involve a two-way interplay between theory and experiment. Theoretical models provide guidance for experimental searches, while experimental results, in turn, constrain theoretical frameworks. A crucial aspect of this feedback loop is the direct inclusion of measurements and exclusion limits ``online'' global fits, i.e. during the parameter scans aspects of the global fits. However, incorporating the Large Hadron Collider (LHC) limits into such analyses has been computationally prohibitive, often due to time taken per parameter point exceeding the scales acceptable for global fit frameworks. In this study, we show that LHC limits can be incorporated ``online'' global fits by leveraging approximations derived from symbolic regression techniques. We utilize a dataset of ATLAS constraints from searches for electroweakino productions to derive a mathematical expression capable of classifying the phenomenological Minimal Supersymmetric Standard Model (pMSSM) parameter space as allowed or excluded. This is subsequently incorporated for making a global fit of the pMSSM to data, including the LHC Run-2 limits.

</details>


### [96] [Explicit soft scale symmetry breaking regulators: the Higgs mass and the consistency of scale symmetry breaking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23713)
*A. R. Vieira*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 显式正则化方案往往人为地破坏标度不变性，导致不必要的物理效应。作者希望基于隐式正则化的条件，寻找不产生这种人为破缺的显式正则子。

Method: 从隐式正则化所确立的条件出发，系统搜寻满足这些条件的显式正则子函数族，并利用拉盖尔多项式对函数族的数学结构进行刻画。

Result: 发现存在一族正则子函数满足所需条件，并能在膨胀流中产生软破缺项；该函数族与拉盖尔多项式存在数学关联。

Conclusion: 该正则化方法被应用于标准模型自然性问题中二次发散的重新审视，可能为处理量子场论中的发散问题提供新的规范途径。

Abstract: In this work, we search for explicit regulators that do not spuriously break scale invariance starting from conditions established by implicit regularization. There is a family of regulator functions that obey the conditions and lead to soft breaking terms of the dilatation current. This family of functions is shown to be related with the Laguerre polynomials. We apply this approach by revisiting quadratic divergences in the context of the naturalness problem of the Standard Model.

</details>

Vankova-Kirilova,N. Vassilopoulos,S. Vihonen,J. Wurtz,V. Zeter,O. Zormpa*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本研究基于图神经网络（GNN）分类器，评估了ESSνSB实验中水切伦科夫（WC）探测器分辨电子与缪子中微子电荷流（CC）事件的能力，并系统研究了探测器缩小体积和降低光电倍增管（PMT）覆盖率对分类性能的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ESSνSB实验需要以高精度测量轻子扇区的电荷宇称（CP）破坏，这要求对水切伦科夫探测器中的中微子事件进行稳健重建。作者希望考察近端WC探测器在缩小或减少仪器配置的情况下是否仍能达到所需的味鉴别性能，以指导探测器设计方案。

Method: 利用中微子带电电流（CC）相互作用的详细蒙特卡洛（Monte Carlo）模拟数据，训练基于图神经网络（GNN）的分类器，用于区分电子中微子CC事件与缪子中微子CC事件，并系统扫描关键探测器设计参数的变体，包括探测器体积和PMT覆盖率。

Result: GNN分类在探测器体积缩小至名义设计的八分之一时仍保持较高准确率，在固定背景抑制率下分类效率仅出现中等程度退化，且该效率损失可通过延长曝光时间大幅补偿。此外，在名义探测器中降低PMT覆盖率对分类性能影响有限，前提是保持高信号产额区域（特别是前向端盖附近）的覆盖率。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，ESSSνSB近端WC探测器可在显著缩小体积或降低PMT覆盖率的情况下维持中微子味鉴别性能，这为探测器设计和实验成本优化提供了重要参考。

Abstract: The ESS$ν$SB experiment aims to measure CP violation in the leptonic sector with high precision, necessitating robust reconstruction of neutrino events in the water Cherenkov (WC) detectors. In this work, we investigate the flavour identification potential of the proposed near WC detector using graph neural network (GNN)-based classification, with a focus on variations of key detector design parameters. In particular, we study whether a smaller and/or less instrumented detector can achieve the required classification performance.
  Using detailed Monte Carlo simulations of charged-current (CC) neutrino interactions, we train GNN classifiers to distinguish electron and muon neutrino CC events. We find that GNN-based classification remains accurate even for detector configurations with volumes up to a factor of eight smaller than the nominal design, with only moderate degradation in classification efficiency at fixed background rejection. The resulting loss in efficiency can largely be compensated by increased exposure time. Furthermore, we demonstrate that reduced photomultiplier tube (PMT) coverage in the nominal detector has a limited impact on classification performance, provided that coverage is maintained in regions of highest signal yield, in particular near the forward end-cap.

</details>


### [98] [Searches for new phenomena in final states with leptons and jets using the ATLAS detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24717)
*Roy Schimmel Brener*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本论文利用大型强子对撞机（Large Hadron Collider, LHC）上的ATLAS探测器，针对标准模型（Standard Model）之外的新物理开展了三个独立的搜寻方向：TeV尺度量子黑洞、破坏味守恒的重共振态，以及Clockwork模型中的周期性引力子信号，分别为这些模型设定了迄今最强的实验上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准模型虽是最精确的科学理论，但并未完整描述所有基本物理现象：无法统一量子力学与引力，无法解释弱能标与普朗克能标之间巨大的层级差距（hierarchy gap），不能解释中微子质量与暗物质（Dark Matter）存在的证据，缺乏足够的重子生成（baryogenesis）机制来解释宇宙中的物质-反物质不对称，并与带电轻子味道普适性（Charged Lepton Flavour Universality）破坏的观测存在张力。这些不足表明存在标准模型之外的新物理。

Method: 论文基于ATLAS探测器数据开展了三项搜寻。第一项针对额外维理论中预言的TeV尺度量子黑洞，利用该模型独有的、随对撞质心能量强烈增强的产生率特性拓展质量搜索范围，采用轻子+喷注（lepton+jet）末态进行分析。第二项针对破坏味守恒的重共振态，使用双轻子+b-喷注末态，对已有包含性结果进行补充并直接约束排他性通道。第三项针对Clockwork模型中周期性出现的重引力子态，在双电子和双光子末态中，将小波变换（wavelet transformations）与机器学习相结合以建立周期性信号搜寻的新方法。

Result: 第一项搜寻为TeV尺度量子黑洞模型设定了迄今最强的质量界限；第二项研究对味破坏共振态提供了排他性通道的直接约束，补充了先前包含性分析；第三项在双电子和双光子末态中验证了结合小波变换与机器学习搜寻周期性信号的新方法，并为Clockwork模型设定了目前最佳的限制范围。

Conclusion: 通过三项互补的新物理搜寻，论文在量子黑洞、味破坏共振态和Clockwork模型三个方向上均取得了迄今最优的实验限制，同时展示了几种新颖的分析策略，包括利用模型唯象特征拓展质量覆盖、以排他性通道补充包含性搜索，以及将机器学习与小波变换结合用于周期性信号探测。

Abstract: The Standard Model is the most precise and consequential theory of science, yet it is not a complete account of all fundamental physics. Amongst its limitations, it lacks a coherent unification of quantum mechanics and gravity, and thus cannot resolve the large hierarchy gap between the weak and Planck scales. It does not explain neutrino masses or the evidence implying the existence of Dark Matter, nor does it provide a viable mechanism for baryogenesis sufficient to explain the observed matter-antimatter asymmetry in the Universe. Lastly, it is in tension with observations hinting toward charged Lepton Flavour Universality violation. These shortcomings indicate that physics beyond the Standard Model must exist. In this thesis, the ATLAS detector at the Large Hadron Collider is used to perform three distinct searches for New Physics. The first targets hypothetical, TeV-scale quantum black holes, which arise in extra-dimensional theories seeking to solve the hierarchy problem. Exploiting a unique feature of the model that strongly enhances the production rate with the collider centre-of-mass energy, and thereby permits a considerable increase in mass reach, it uses lepton+jet final states and sets the strongest limits on the model to date. The second tackles anomalies in lepton flavour by searching for heavy resonances that violate flavour conservation; targeting dilepton+$b$-jets final states, it complements previous inclusive results by directly addressing exclusive ones. The third targets Clockwork, periodic signatures of heavy graviton states appearing in extra-dimensional models. Performed in dielectron and diphoton final states, it establishes a new method to search for periodic signals using wavelet transformations with machine learning, and sets the best limits on the model.

</details>


### [99] [Cosmic muon arrival directions as a source of entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24729)
*Deepak Samuel*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文首次证明由缪子望远镜（muon telescope）探测到的宇宙线缪子（cosmic-ray muons）的到达方向可作为物理熵（physical entropy）的可测量来源。基于六个月的实验数据，利用缪子径迹的本地天顶角与方位角进行随机性提取，每条缪子径迹可产生最多4比特（bits）随机数，熵值达0.999182比特/比特，最大比特率约为650比特/秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的随机数生成器（random number generator）可能无法完全满足贝尔不等式（Bell's inequality）违逆实验中设置独立性漏洞（settings-independence loophole）闭合的要求，需要寻找具有内在物理随机性的熵来源。

Method: 利用缪子望远镜收集六个月的实验数据，提取每条宇宙线缪子径迹的本地天顶角（zenith angle）与方位角（azimuth angle）进行随机性抽取（randomness extraction），并设计了完整的数据采集（data acquisition）、数据约减（data reduction）与调理（conditioning）算法。

Result: 每条缪子径迹可产出最多4比特，熵值（entropy）达到0.999182比特/比特，最大比特率约为650比特/秒；提高探测器尺寸与角分辨率（angular resolution）可进一步提升该比特率。

Conclusion: 宇宙线缪子到达方向可作为可信的物理熵源，该技术有望用于闭合贝尔不等式检验中的设置独立性漏洞，作为随机数生成器的潜在实现方案。

Abstract: We demonstrate, for the first time, that the arrival directions of cosmic-ray muons from a muon telescope provide a measurable source of physical entropy. Using six months of data collected from a muon telescope, the local zenith and azimuth angles were used for randomness extraction. The result of this study show that each cosmic muon track from our detector can generate upto 4 bits with an entropy of 0.999182 bits/bit with a maximum bit rate of roughly 650 bits/s which can possibly be enhanced by improving the detector size and angular resolution. The suggested technique can be potentially used as a random number generator required for closing the settings-independence loophole in the tests for violations of Bell's inequality. The data acquisition, data reduction, and conditioning algorithms are discussed in detail.

</details>


### [100] [Expected performance of a water Cherenkov detector for reactor antineutrino--electron scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24746)
*Sunhong Kim,Sunwoo Gwon,Youngju Ko,Hokyeong Nam,Juseong Park,Yujin Park,Kim Siyeon*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文评估了在RENO近探测器站点部署一个170吨水切伦科夫探测器，用于测量反应堆反中微子—电子弹性散射的预期性能。基于365.25天的曝光，通过约束轮廓似然拟合从重建反冲电子方向分布中提取反应堆能谱归一化因子，结果表明水切伦科夫探测器可以提供百分级灵敏度的方向信息确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在评估利用水切伦科夫探测器在水反应堆近场站点测量反中微子—电子弹性散射的可行性，特别是利用方向信息来约束反应堆能谱归一化因子，以检验该技术路径是否具备充足灵敏度。

Method: 采用带约束的轮廓似然拟合（constrained profile-likelihood fit）方法，从重建的反冲电子方向分布中提取反应堆能谱归一化因子，并在两种场景下进行分析：最小触发击中数N_prompt≥8（不考虑PMT放射性）和N_prompt≥20（包含模拟PMT放射性成分），曝光时间为365.25天。

Result: 在N_prompt≥8场景下，反应堆能谱归一化因子的总不确定度为+1.08%/-1.07%；在更严格的N_prompt≥20场景（包含PMT放射性）下，总不确定度为+1.49%/-1.47%。

Conclusion: 来自水切伦科夫探测器的方向信息可以为反应堆弹性散射归一化因子提供百分级（percent-level）灵敏度，表明该技术方案在反应堆反中微子测量中具有可行性。

Abstract: We evaluate the expected performance of a 170-ton water Cherenkov detector for reactor antineutrino--electron elastic scattering at the RENO near-detector site. The reactor-spectrum normalization is extracted from the reconstructed recoil-electron directional distribution using a constrained profile-likelihood fit for an exposure of 365.25 days. For the minimum prompt-hit multiplicity $N_{\mathrm{prompt}}\geq8$ scenario, which neglects PMT radioactivity, the total uncertainty on the reactor-spectrum normalization is ${}^{+1.08\%}_{-1.07\%}$. For the more restrictive $N_{\mathrm{prompt}}\geq20$ scenario, which includes the simulated PMT-radioactivity components, the total uncertainty is ${}^{+1.49\%}_{-1.47\%}$. These results demonstrate that directional information from a water Cherenkov detector can provide percent-level sensitivity to the reactor elastic-scattering normalization.

</details>


### [101] [Binary Hypothesis Testing: A Robust Framework Against the Look Elsewhere Effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24872)
*Han Zhang,Xue-feng Ding,Yu-Feng Li,Yi-fang Wang,Liang-jian Wen,Liang Zhan*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文重新诠释了粒子物理中的"look-elsewhere效应"，将其从简单的扫描惩罚重新定义为一种对程序不一致性的校正，并论证了二元检验对该效应稳健，而峰搜索则需要显式校正，并基于ATLAS希格斯搜索校准的试验因子（trial factor）约26，给出了3σ全局显著性与4σ二元检验显著性对应的数值关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在粒子物理中，5σ显著性是发现判定的惯例标准，但其解读高度依赖于检验程序——是单点预设检验还是参数空间中扫描检验。look-elsewhere效应虽被广泛使用，但常被简化为扫描惩罚，缺乏清晰的统计诠释，需要重新审视其本质。

Method: 在假设检验（hypothesis testing）框架内，将look-elsewhere效应重新定义为"当零分布在一套程序下生成而检验统计量在另一套程序下评估时"所产生的程序不一致性（procedural inconsistency）的校正。区分了二元检验（binary test）与峰搜索（peak search）两种场景下该效应的不同角色，并以ATLAS希格斯搜索校准的中等试验因子（约26）进行了定量演示。

Result: 二元检验对look-elsewhere效应是稳健的，而峰搜索需要对信号位置搜索进行显式校正。利用约26的试验因子，同一观测检验统计量下，峰搜索的3σ全局显著性（global significance）约对应于二元检验的4σ显著性。

Conclusion: 该重新表述为look-elsewhere效应提供了更清晰的统计诠释，并为粒子物理中的显著性声明提供了一个更连贯的框架，有助于统一理解不同检验程序下的显著性含义。

Abstract: In particle physics, discovery claims conventionally require an observed significance exceeding $5σ$. However, the interpretation of a $5σ$ result depends critically on the testing procedure, namely whether the hypothesis is tested at a single pre-specified point in parameter space or by scanning over a range of possible signal locations. This distinction gives rise to the look-elsewhere effect, a concept that is widely used but often interpreted as a simple penalty for scanning. In this work, we reinterpret the look-elsewhere effect as a correction for procedural inconsistency arising when the null distribution is generated under one procedure while the test statistic is evaluated under another. Within the framework of hypothesis testing, we examine its implications and clarify the distinct roles of the look-elsewhere effect in binary and peak-search scenarios. In particular, we show that binary test is robust against the look-elsewhere effect, whereas peak searches require an explicit correction for the search over signal locations. Using a moderate trial factor of approximately 26, calibrated from the ATLAS Higgs search, we show that a $3σ$ global significance of peak search can correspond to approximately $4σ$ significance of the binary test for the same value of the observed test statistic. This reformulation provides a clearer statistical interpretation of the look-elsewhere effect and offers a more coherent framework for understanding significance claims in particle physics.

</details>

### [102] [Optimal Hardy Inequalities for Random Walks on $\mathbb{Z}^2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24213)
*Philipp Hake,Matthias Keller,Felix Pogorzelski*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文证明了在 $\mathbb{Z}^2$ 上每个非周期、对称且具有有限方差的随机游走均满足最优 Hardy 不等式（Hardy inequality），并验证了其 Hardy 权重的零临界性（null-criticality）。通过 Potkin 核的渐近分析，作者推导出权重的渐近行为，并证明标准拉普拉斯算子情形下 Hardy 不等式常数不超过连续情形的最优常数 $1/4$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hardy 不等式（Hardy inequality）在分析学与概率论中具有基础地位，其在离散图（如 $\mathbb{Z}^d$）上的最优常数问题长期以来未被完全解决。将最优 Hardy 不等式推广到一般非周期随机游走情形，并验证最优常数，有助于统一并推广已有结果（如 Kapitanski 与 Laptev 的经典工作）。

Method: 作者利用 Fukai 与 Uchiyama 给出的势核（potential kernel）精细渐近分析来推导 Hardy 权重的渐近形式；同时提出一个新的适用于超越局部有限（locally finite）情形的通用图判定准则，用以证明零临界性。该新准则同时适用于 $\mathbb{Z}^d$（$d \geq 3$）的情形。

Result: 证明了 $\mathbb{Z}^2$ 上所有非周期、对称、有限方差随机游走的 Hardy 权重最优性（零临界性）；对标准拉普拉斯算子恢复了一阶渐近项，并证明次阶项为负，从而表明 Kapitanski–Laptev 所证 Hardy 不等式常数不可能大于连续情形的最优常数 $1/4$。

Conclusion: 该研究为离散 Hardy 不等式的最优常数问题提供了统一处理框架，将结论从 $\mathbb{Z}^2$ 与 $\mathbb{Z}^d$（$d \geq 3$）推广到更一般的图结构，并证实离散情形与连续情形在最优常数上的一致性是紧密且非平凡的。

Abstract: We prove an optimal Hardy inequality for every aperiodic, symmetric random walk in $\mathbb{Z}^2$ with finite variance. In particular, we verify null-criticality, and thus, optimality of the underlying Hardy weight. Under suitable moment conditions, we use fine asymptotics of the potential kernel due to Fukai and Uchiyama in order to derive the asymptotics of the weight. For the standard Laplacian, we recover the expected first order term in the asymptotics but also show that next order term is negative. Thus, our result shows that the constant in the Hardy inequality proven by Kapitanski and Laptev cannot be larger than $1/4$, which is the optimal constant in the continuum. The proof of null-criticality rests on a new criterion for general graphs beyond the locally finite case. We also recover the situation of $\mathbb{Z}^d$ with $d \geq 3$ which can also be also treated by our new method.

</details>


<div id='math.OA'></div>

# math.OA [[Back]](#toc)

### [103] [A genus zero functorial CFT associated to the vacuum sector of a conformal net](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24437)
*André G. Henriques,James E. Tener*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 本文从任意共形网（conformal net）出发，构造了一个属零的函子共形场论（genus zero functorial conformal field theory），其希尔伯特空间（Hilbert space）为网的真空扇区（vacuum sector），并由此证明了所有共形网满足迹类条件（trace class condition）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该工作的动机在于建立共形网这一数学对象与函子共形场论之间的明确联系，并通过这种构造推导出共形网的普适性质（如迹类条件），从而深化对共形场论底层结构的理解。

Method: 作者构造了作用于小平盘（little discs）operad 的代数以及共形嵌入（conformal embeddings），其取值在希尔伯特空间与有界线性映射（bounded linear maps）构成的范畴中。工作中使用闭盘，并明确允许入射盘（incoming disc）的像与出射盘（outgoing disc）的边界重合重叠。

Result: 作者证明了所有共形网均满足迹类条件：若 $L_0$ 为网的共形哈密顿量（conformal Hamiltonian），则对任意 $0 \le r < 1$，算子 $r^{L_0}$ 均为迹类算子。特别地，共形网的 $L_0$ 特征空间（eigenspaces）自动为有限维。

Conclusion: 该构造不仅建立了从共形网到函子共形场论的系统性框架，还给出了一个普适的有限维性结论，表明共形网在真空扇区上的能量级数必然是精细的。

Abstract: Starting from an arbitrary conformal net, we construct a genus zero functorial conformal field theory whose Hilbert space is the vacuum sector of the net. Specifically, we construct an algebra for the operad of little discs and conformal embeddings with values in the category of Hilbert spaces and bounded linear maps. We work with closed discs, and our conformal embeddings explicitly allow the image of an incoming disc to overlap with the boundary of the outgoing disc. As an application, we show that all conformal nets satisfy the trace class condition: if $L_0$ is the conformal Hamiltonian of the net, then the operators $r^{L_0}$ are trace class whenever $0 \le r < 1$. In particular, the $L_0$-eigenspaces of a conformal net are automatically finite-dimensional.

</details>

### [104] [Direct linearization, Cauchy matrix and Sato Grassmannian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24538)
*Kanehisa Takasaki*

Main category: nlin.SI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了Fu和Nijhoff提出的KP层次（KP hierarchy）直接线性化方案与佐藤格拉斯曼流形（Sato Grassmannian）之间的几何联系，证明其部分矩阵元对应于佐藤格拉斯曼流形顶部胞腔的仿射坐标。通过引入负流（negative flows），该系统的几何解释可扩展到其他矩阵元，且扩展系统与双分量KP层次本质等价。柯西矩阵方法、多分量推广（AKNS和ASDYM层次）均可在此几何视角下统一理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在为KP层次及其约化的直接线性化方案提供清晰的几何解释，揭示该代数方法背后的几何结构，并探索其与已知可积系统（如双分量KP、AKNS、ASDYM层次）之间深刻的联系。

Method: 作者将Fu和Nijhoff的无穷矩阵U的演化方程与佐藤格拉斯曼流形上的KP层次几何描述建立对应关系，通过引入负流扩展该系统，从而将几何解释推广到U的全部矩阵元，并与双分量KP层次建立等价；进一步利用该几何框架重新阐释柯西矩阵方法，并通过多分量推广将AKNS与ASDYM层次关联到多分量佐藤格拉斯曼流形。

Result: 研究表明，U的矩阵元演化方程与佐藤格拉斯曼流形顶部胞腔仿射坐标wij的演化方程完全一致；引入负流后的扩展系统与双分量KP层次本质等价；柯西矩阵方法可由该几何视角自然解释；多分量推广的Fu-Nijhoff非线性系统分别联系AKNS和ASDYM层次，并以多分量佐藤格拉斯曼流形作为其几何结构。

Conclusion: 本文建立的几何框架统一了直接线性化方案、佐藤格拉斯曼流形与多种可积层次之间的联系，彰显了佐藤格拉斯曼流形及其多分量推广作为可积系统几何基础的普适性。

Abstract: Fu and Nijhoff's direct linearization scheme for the KP hierarchy and its reductions employs a system of evolution equations of an infinite matrix $U$ with quadratic nonlinearity. Part of the matrix elements of $U$ can be identified with affine coordinates $w_{ij}$ of the top cell of the Sato Grassmannian. The evolution equations of these matrix elements are identical to the evolution equations of $w_{ij}$ representing the KP hierarchy in geometric terms. This geometric interpretation can be extended to other matrix elements of $U$ by introducing negative flows to the evolution equations of $U$. The extended system turns out to be substantially equivalent to the two-component KP hierarchy. The Cauchy matrix approach to the KP hierarchy can be explained in this geometric perspective. Multi-component generalizations of Fu and Nijhoff's nonlinear system are related to the AKNS and ASDYM hierarchies. Multi-component Sato Grassmannians show up therein as the relevant geometric structure.

</details>

### [105] [Constraints on Rastall gravity from current observational data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23594)
*Mahnaz Asghari,Hooman Moradpour*

Main category: physics.gen-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了Rastall引力（Rastall gravity）的宇宙学特征——在该理论中，协变能量-动量守恒被修改为弯曲时空中的$\nabla_\mu T^\mu_\nu = \lambda \nabla_\nu R$。通过获得该模型到线性扰动阶的修正场方程，作者检查了物质功率谱（matter power spectrum）和哈勃参数（Hubble parameter）等宇宙学可观测量的演化行为，并与CMB、弱透镜、超新星、重子声学振荡和红移空间畸变等宇宙学观测数据进行比较。数值结果表明Rastall引力比$\Lambda$CDM模型偏好更低的结构增长，与低红移大尺度结构探针一致；同时，以宇宙学常数作为暗能量组分的Rastall引力同样遭受哈勃张力（Hubble tension）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Rastall引力是对广义相对论的一种修正，其中能量-动量张量的协变守恒被修改，从而引入了新的宇宙学效应。本文旨在通过线性扰动分析和与多种宇宙学观测数据的比较，系统评估Rastall引力能否为当前宇宙学中已知的结构增长异常和哈勃张力等问题提供替代解释。

Method: 作者首先推导Rastall模型在线性扰动阶的修正场方程，然后数值求解并考察物质功率谱与哈勃参数等宇宙学可观测量的演化行为；随后将Rastall引力模型与多种宇宙学探针数据（包含宇宙微波背景、弱透镜、超新星、重子声学振荡及红移空间畸变）进行统计比较。

Result: 数值结果显示，相较于$\Lambda$CDM模型，Rastall引力倾向于更低的结构增长（structure growth），这与低红移大尺度结构探针的观测保持一致。此外，当以宇宙学常数作为暗能量组分时，Rastall引力同样面临哈勃张力问题，其严重程度类似于$\Lambda$CDM模型。

Conclusion: Rastall引力在低红移结构增长方面与观测数据具有一致性，显示出其作为$\Lambda$CDM模型潜在替代方案的可行性；然而，该模型并未缓解哈勃张力问题，这暗示其对当前宇宙学紧张关系的解决能力有限。

Abstract: We study the cosmological features of Rastall gravity, where the covariant energy-momentum conservation is modified to $\nabla_μT^μ_ν=λ\nabla_νR$ in curved spacetime. For this purpose, we obtain the modified field equations of Rastall model to linear order of perturbations, and then inspect the evolutionary behavior of cosmological observables, chiefly the matter power spectrum and the Hubble parameter, within the context of Rastall gravity. We also compare Rastall model with cosmological probes, namely cosmic microwave background, weak lensing, supernovae, baryon acoustic oscillations, and redshift-space distortions data. According to our numerical results, Rastall gravity prefers lower growth of structures compared to the $Λ$CDM model, which indicates consistency with low-redshift large-scale structure probes. Moreover, numerical analysis reveals that Rastall gravity with a cosmological constant as the dark energy component suffers from the Hubble tension, like the $Λ$CDM model.

</details>

### [106] [Universal Spin-Position Coupled Rydberg Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23694)
*Chengshu Li,Hui Zhai*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文发现当两个s轨道里德堡原子的主量子数相差一个"甜点值"（sweet-spot value）时，可以涌现出强电子自旋依赖的里德堡相互作用。该相互作用源于邻近p轨道的精细结构分裂（fine-structure splitting），具有独特的对称性质，使电子自旋与两原子相对位置耦合，展现出空间定义的各向异性。作者证明这种相互作用具有不依赖于主量子数的普适形式，与传统的磁偶极相互作用有根本区别，是一种自然界中新型的本征磁相互作用；并以此为基础提出了基塔耶夫-海森堡模型（Kitaev-Heisenberg model）的本征实现方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: s轨道里德堡原子之间的强相互作用是原子阵列量子计算和量子模拟的基础，但这些相互作用的电子自旋依赖性通常可忽略。作者旨在探索是否可能存在显著的电子自旋依赖的里德堡相互作用，并揭示其物理机制与潜在应用。

Method: 作者从理论分析出发，考察两个s轨道里德堡原子主量子数存在特定差值时的相互作用，揭示其来源于附近p轨道的精细结构分裂，并分析相互作用对称性质、普适形式及其与磁偶极相互作用的区别。

Result: 发现了一种强电子自旋依赖的里德堡相互作用，具有空间各向异性和普适形式；建立了它与传统磁偶极相互作用的根本区别，并展示了其在里德堡量子模拟工具箱中引入新元素，具体实现了基塔耶夫-海森堡模型，该模型中出现了自旋空间锁定（spin-space locking）的量子多体表现——一种不寻常的条纹相（stripe phase）。

Conclusion: 该研究确立了自然界中一种新型的本征磁相互作用类型，为里德堡量子模拟开辟了新方向，并通过基塔耶夫-海森堡模型的具体实现展示了其潜在应用价值。

Abstract: Strong interactions between $s$-orbital Rydberg atoms underpin atom-array-based quantum computation and quantum simulation. Typically, the spin dependence of these interactions is negligible and plays no practical role. In this Letter, we uncover that a strong electron-spin-dependent Rydberg interaction can emerge when the principal quantum numbers of the two $s$-orbital atoms differ by a sweet-spot value. This interaction originates from the fine-structure splitting of nearby $p$ orbitals, which endows it with distinct symmetry properties such that the electron spins are coupled to the relative position of the two atoms, featuring a spatially defined anisotropy. We further demonstrate that this interaction admits a universal form, independent of the principal quantum numbers, and highlight its fundamental distinction from conventional magnetic dipolar interactions, establishing it as a new type of native magnetic interaction in nature. Our findings introduce a new element to the Rydberg quantum simulation toolbox. As a concrete application, we propose a native realization of the Kitaev-Heisenberg model, which hosts an unusual stripe phase as a quantum many-body manifestation of spin-space locking.

</details>


### [107] [Observation of electron spin interactions between Rydberg atoms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23711)
*Le Ruan,Ziqi Zhou,Yuchen Guo,Chengshu Li,Cheng Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 该研究首次观测到里德伯原子（Rydberg atoms）间的电子自旋相互作用，该相互作用由自旋-轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）通过二阶偶极微扰驱动，并表现出由原子构型决定的空间各向异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展里德伯原子阵列在量子模拟（quantum simulation）领域的应用范围，探索新型电子自旋-自旋相互作用机制。

Method: 通过数值计算与理论模型对比验证耦合强度；利用全局微波缀饰（microwave dressing）引入差分AC斯塔克位移（AC Stark shift）来工程化和动态冻结自旋交换；施加更强磁场调节自旋-轨道耦合通道的能量贡献以实现可调性；在一维多原子链中分别沿平行和垂直于磁场方向测量自旋动力学，对XXZ各向异性模型进行自洽验证。

Result: 观测到相干电子自旋交换动力学，耦合强度与数值计算和理论模型一致；实现了相互作用的空间各向异性控制及磁场可调性；平行与垂直磁场方向的多原子链测量结果自洽验证了各向异性XXZ框架。

Conclusion: 该电子自旋相互作用天然具备自旋-位置耦合（spin-position coupling）特性，可自然映射到海森堡-基塔耶夫模型（Heisenberg-Kitaev model），为里德伯原子阵列的量子模拟开辟了新方向。

Abstract: We report the observation of electron spin interactions between Rydberg atoms, which are driven by spin-orbit coupling through second-order dipole perturbation and exhibit a spatial anisotropy governed by the atomic configuration. Specifically, we observe coherent electron spin exchange dynamics, with the measured coupling strength agreeing well with both numerical calculations and theoretical models. Furthermore, we show that global microwave dressing enables active engineering and dynamical freezing of the spin exchange by introducing a differential AC Stark shift between the participating states. Additionally, we achieve tunability of the interaction by applying a stronger magnetic field, which effectively modifies the energy contributions of the underlying spin-orbit coupling channels. Finally, measuring spin dynamics in one-dimensional multi-atom chains aligned parallel or perpendicular to the magnetic field provides a self-consistent validation of the anisotropic XXZ framework. This electron spin interaction natively features spin-position coupling and enables a natural mapping onto the Heisenberg-Kitaev model. Our findings reveal a new class of electron spin-spin interactions among Rydberg atoms, expanding the scope of quantum simulation with Rydberg atom arrays.

</details>


### [108] [Tunable Mediated Interactions Near Spontaneous Symmetry Breaking: From Yukawa to Coulomb and Dzyaloshinskii--Moriya Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23954)
*Hoshu Hiyane,Hiroyuki Tajima*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种由自发对称性破缺产生的南布-戈德斯通模（Nambu-Goldstone mode）所介导的可调谐长程相互作用实现方案，可分别呈现汤川型（Yukawa-type）短程相互作用、库仑型（Coulomb）相互作用或类似磁化向量耦合（Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya-like cross product coupling）的多种形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多量子多体系统中非局域相互作用（non-local interactions）的可控实现是量子模拟的关键挑战。作者旨在利用自发对称性破缺系统中固有的NG模作为媒介粒子（bath particles），以实现具有可调特性的长程有效相互作用，为自旋模型和极化子（polarons）的量子模拟提供新的物理机制。

Method: 作者考虑两个可区分杂质（impurities）浸入在具有自发对称性破缺的系统中，杂质间的有效交换相互作用正比于由介质粒子介导的推迟磁化率（retarded susceptibility）。NG模的来源包括：排斥性的双组分费米气体或玻色气体中的磁振子模（magnon mode），以及玻色-费米混合物中的金斯蒂诺模（goldstino mode）。通过调节对称性破缺的显式性（explicit vs. spontaneous）来调控NG模的能隙，从而实现对相互作用形式（Yukawa型、Coulomb型或长程类Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya耦合）的控制。

Result: 当NG模被显式对称性破缺打开能隙时，杂质间相互作用呈现Yukawa型短程形式；在无能隙极限下则呈现库仑定律形式；在NG模阈值（threshold）以上，出现长程Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya型叉积耦合。

Conclusion: 该方案为实现具有可控非局域相互作用的自旋模型和极化子量子模拟器铺平了道路。

Abstract: We propose a realization of tunable long-range mediated interactions generated by Nambu-Goldstone (NG) modes near spontaneous symmetry breaking. An effective exchange interaction emerges between two distinguishable impurities proportional to the retarded susceptibility mediated by the bath particles. The interaction is mediated by emergent NG modes associated with symmetry breaking in systems, namely the magnon mode in a repulsive two-component Fermi or Bose gas, or the goldstino mode in a Bose-Fermi mixture. When the NG mode is gapped by explicit symmetry breaking, the interaction has a Yukawa-type short-ranged interaction, whereas the gapless limit gives a Coulomb law. Above the NG mode threshold, the Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya-like cross product coupling emerges in a long-range form. Our proposal paves a way towards the realization of a quantum simulator of spin models and polarons with controllable non-local interactions.

</details>


### [109] [Roton Instability in Quantum Droplets with Finite-Range Soft-Core Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24813)
*Avra Banerjee*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文通过扩展Gross-Pitaevskii方程（extended Gross-Pitaevskii equation）研究软芯势（soft-core potential）相互作用下自束缚量子液滴（self-bound quantum droplets）中的旋子不稳定性（roton instability），揭示旋子最小值的软化直至系统失稳并触发密度调制的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子液滴中旋子激发（roton excitation）的涌现机制尚待深入理解，特别是有限程软芯势如何驱动旋子不稳定性。本研究旨在通过求解含Lee-Huang-Yang（LHY）修正的非局域相互作用模型，系统刻画该现象。

Method: 采用Heaviside阶跃相互作用（Heaviside step interaction）模拟有限程软芯势，求解包含LHY修正和非局域相互作用项的扩展Gross-Pitaevskii方程以获得基态性质，并在此基础上计算集体激发谱（collective excitation spectrum）和静态结构因子（static structure factor）。

Result: 随着相互作用强度和范围的增大，旋子最小值逐步形成并持续软化；当旋子能量趋近于零时，系统失稳，标志着密度调制（density modulation）的 onset。静态结构因子在旋子动量处呈现显著峰。

Conclusion: 有限程软芯势量子液滴中旋子不稳定性是密度调制的前驱信号，其标志性特征为激发谱旋子软化及静态结构因子的峰值响应。

Abstract: We investigate the emergence of roton instability in self-bound quantum droplets interacting via a finite-range soft-core potential modeled by a Heaviside step interaction. The ground-state properties are obtained by solving the extended Gross-Pitaevskii equation including Lee-Huang-Yang corrections with a nonlocal interaction term. The collective excitation spectrum reveals the formation and progressive softening of a roton minimum as the interaction strength and range increase. When the roton energy approaches zero, the system becomes unstable, signaling the onset of density modulation. The rotonic behavior is further characterized through the static structure factor, which exhibits pronounced peaks at the roton momentum.

</details>

### [110] [A General Response Theory for Closure Phases through Visibility Nulls and Image Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23690)
*Shokoufe Faraji,Avery E. Broderick*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文首次为任意亮度分布和任意零自由观测三角形建立了闭合相位（closure phase）的统一响应理论，将闭合相位视为对数可见度（log-visibility）可观测量，推导出图像扰动的精确一阶主核和笛卡尔矩层级，并识别了真可见度零点处理论的奇异边界；该框架已应用于EHT对M87*的观测数据，并自然延伸到光学和中红外干涉测量中的相位闭合消零（Phase Closure Nulling）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闭合相位对台站相位误差具有鲁棒性，但其与图像结构的联系往往仅被定性解释，非零闭合相位主要依赖对称性论证或特定源模型来理解。缺乏一种统一、定量的理论框架来将闭合相位结构转化为对任意亮度分布下微弱和非对称源特征的定量探测手段。

Method: 将闭合相位重新表述为对数可见度观测量（log-visibility observable），由此推导出图像扰动的精确一阶主核（master kernel）及笛卡尔矩层级（Cartesian moment hierarchy），将源变化的空间内容与观测三角形的灵敏度分离；同时建立傅里叶平面位移的局部展开以描述沿观测轨道的闭合相位演化。进一步分析真可见度零点处的边界行为，依据零点阶数（order）和绕数电荷（winding charge）区分可移除的相位缠绕、非零可见度极小值和可见度相位多值的零点，并用结构化环形模型验证展开，最后应用到M87*的EHT数据和干涉测量。

Result: 该理论在非零点区域提供精确的解析展开，在零点区域刻画了相位行为的局部控制机制；通过结构化环形模型验证了展开的有效性，并利用M87*的EHT数据展示了直接从闭合相位演化中测量与高阶图像矩相关的不对称图像结构的能力。

Conclusion: 该统一框架将闭合相位结构转变为跨干涉测量体制的定量探针，能够探测微弱且不对称的源特征，并自然扩展到光学和红外干涉测量中的相位闭合消零技术。

Abstract: Closure phase is robust to station based phase errors, yet its connection to image structure is often interpreted only qualitatively and a nonzero closure phase is still interpreted mainly through qualitative symmetry arguments or source specific models. We develop, to our knowledge, the first unified response theory for an arbitrary brightness distribution and an arbitrary zero free observing triangle. Away from visibility zeros, closure phase is naturally a log-visibility observable. This structure yields an exact first order master kernel for image perturbations and a Cartesian moment hierarchy that separates the spatial content of the source change from the sensitivity of the sampled triangle. For a fixed image, the same framework gives a local expansion in Fourier plane displacement, providing a direct description of closure phase evolution along observing tracks. We then identify the boundary of this regular regime: at a true visibility null, the log-visibility expansion fails and the phase behavior is controlled by the local properties of the null, including its order and winding charge, distinguishing removable phase wraps, non-null visibility minima, and nulls for which the visibility phase is multivalued. We validate these expansions using structured ring models. We then apply both branches of the theory to EHT data of M87*, and demonstrate the ability to measure asymmetric image structure associated with higher image moments directly from the evolution of the closure phase. Finally, we show that the same formalism extends naturally to Phase Closure Nulling in optical and infrared interferometry. The resulting framework turns closure phase structure into a quantitative probe of faint and asymmetric source features across interferometric regimes.

</details>
