### [111] [Tunable Magnetic Frustration in the Cu-Ru-based Double Perovskite La$_{2-x}$Sm$_x$CuRuO$_6$ (x = 0, 1, 2) Oxides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23741)
*Soumya Ghorai,Samir Rom,Irina Shamova,N. K. Karn,Tamanna Kumari,A. K. Shukla,Sanjoy Kr. Mahatha,O. Volkova,Nitesh Kumar Tanusri Saha Dasgupta,Setti Thirupathaiah*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究通过固相反应法制备了铜钌酸盐氧化物双钙钛矿 La₂₋ₓSmₓCuRuO₆（x = 0, 1, 2），并联合实验与第一性原理计算，揭示了晶格畸变与稀土离子磁性对磁挫败（magnetic frustration）的调控作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铜-钌双钙钛矿材料中晶体结构与磁性之间存在复杂的相互作用，理解其调控机制对于设计可调磁性能的功能材料至关重要。

Method: 采用固相反应法合成样品，并运用X射线衍射（XRD）、电阻率测量、交流磁化率、热容测量及第一性原理计算等方法，系统表征样品的结构、磁性和电子性质。

Result: 所有化合物均结晶为单斜对称（monoclinic symmetry），且随着Sm³⁺替代La³⁺，结构畸变程度增大；所有样品呈现绝缘行为，低温下以变程跳跃（variable-range hopping）导电为主，源于反位无序（anti-site disorder）；含Sm化合物中磁挫败受到抑制，实验观测与第一性原理计算一致。

Conclusion: 晶格畸变与Sm磁性共同作用削弱了磁挫败，从而合理化实验观测结果；本研究揭示了Cu-Ru双钙钛矿中晶体结构与磁性的复杂关联，并为通过稀土离子替换调控磁性能提供了新途径。

Abstract: In this study, we investigate structural, magnetic, and electronic properties of the copper-ruthenate based oxide double perovskite La$_{2-x}$Sm$_x$CuRuO$_6$ (x = 0, 1, 2), synthesized through the solid-state reaction method. X-ray diffraction analysis reveals that all compounds crystallize in a monoclinic symmetry, with varying degree of structural distortion that increases in moving from La$^{3+}$ to smaller size cation Sm$^{3+}$. Electrical resistivity studies indicate insulating behaviour in all compounds, with variable-range-hopping domination at low temperatures, due to presence of anti-site disorder. AC susceptibility and heat capacity measurements suggest suppression of frustration in Sm-bearing compounds, affecting the magnetic behavior. Our first-principles calculations suggest that the combined effects of lattice distortion and Sm magnetism play a crucial role in weakening magnetic frustration, thereby rationalizing the experimental observations. These findings shed light on the complex interplay of crystal structure and magnetism in Cu-Ru double perovskites, and open up an avenue for tuning of magnetic properties through rare-earth-ion substitution.

</details>


### [112] [Can Strain or Anion Interchange Make an Unstable Structure Stable? Energetics, Lattice Dynamics and Strain-Tunable Band Gaps of Lithium Chalcohalide Antiperovskites (Li$_{3}$$BA$) and their Anion Interchange Variants (Li$_{3}$$AB$)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23815)
*Ismail A. Buliyaminu,Ehsan Gowdini,Phillip Duxbury,Jose L. Mendoza-Cortes*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过第一性原理计算系统研究了锂硫卤反钙钛矿（lithium chalcohalide antiperovskites）中阴离子互换机制，揭示了较小的阴离子位于八面体内部时结构更稳定，并发现部分化合物可通过三轴压缩应变实现动力学稳定，同时应变可有效调控电子带隙以适应固态电池应用需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 锂硫卤反钙钛矿是铅卤钙钛矿的非毒性替代材料，有潜力用作锂离子电池固态电解质，但其阴离子互换机制尚未被充分探索，本文旨在系统研究该机制对结构稳定性和电子性质的影响。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算，对立方反钙钛矿Li₃BA（A=O, S, Se, Te, Po；B=F, Cl, Br, I）进行阴离子互换计算，分析相对能量景观、晶格动力学（声子谱）、电子结构与态密度（density of states, DOS），并通过三轴压缩应变研究其动力学稳定性与带隙调控。

Result: 计算结果揭示：八面体内部较小的阴离子有利于结构稳定；Li₃FA（A=Te, Po）和Li₃OB（B=Cl, Br, I）为最优能量上稳定且无虚频的动态稳定立方相；Li₃FS和Li₃FSe虽能量稳定但在平衡态下动力学不稳定，需施加三轴压缩应变方可稳定；阴离子互换导致带隙显著变化，少量应变可将带隙调至固态电池电化学稳定窗口内。

Conclusion: 本研究揭示了阴离子位点互换机制的关键特征，为锂硫卤反钙钛矿的理解与设计提供了理论基础，并展示了应变工程在固态电池应用中的调控潜力。

Abstract: Lithium chalcohalide antiperovskites are a promising, non-toxic alternative to lead halide perovskites, with potential as solid electrolytes for Li-ion batteries. we computationally investigate a relatively unexplored anion-interchange mechanism by which cubic Li$_{3}$$AB$ derivatives are obtained from the parent cubic antiperovskite Li$_{3}$$BA$ ($A$ = O, S, Se, Te, Po; $B$ = F, Cl, Br, I). The calculated relative energy landscape provides a useful guide for anion-site selectivity and its role in structural stability. The energetic stability results reveal that the smaller anion inside the octahedron stabilizes the structures. The lattice-dynamic calculations confirm that Li$_{3}$F$A$ ($A$ = Te, Po) and Li$_{3}$O$B$ ($B$ = Cl, Br, I), which are the most energetically stable compounds, are dynamically stable cubic phases without imaginary phonon modes. However, Li$_{3}$FS and Li$_{3}$FSe, while energetically stable, are dynamically unstable at equilibrium and become dynamically stable under triaxial compressive strain. In addition, we report the electronic structure and density of states (DOS) of all compounds, which show a substantial change in band gap upon anion interchange. The strain engineering of the lithium chalcohalide family illustrates how a few percent of the strain can tune the electronic band gap within the electrochemical stability window for solid battery applications. This study unveils essential characteristics of the anion site-interchange mechanism and provides a foundation for the understanding and design of lithium chalcohalide antiperovskites.

</details>


### [113] [Spectrally Programmable Spin-Polarized Photocurrents in WSe$_2$-NiPS$_3$ Magnetic van der Waals Heterostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23832)
*Rajesh Kumar Yadav,Michal Poplinger,Adi Levi,Adi Harchol,Nirman Chakraborty,Thomas Brumme,Thomas Heine,Efrat Lifshitz,Doron Naveh*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种基于NiPS₃/WSe₂范德华异质结（van der Waals heterostructure）的材料平台，通过圆偏振光激发在p-n二极管结构中实现光谱可编程的自旋输运（spectrally programmable spin transport），自旋极化率在尼尔温度（Néel temperature）附近达到80%、室温下保持30%，且无需磁场即可产生自旋极化电流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半导体中自旋极化电流的高效产生与控制是自旋电子学（spintronics）的核心挑战，现有方法受限于阻抗失配（impedance mismatch）以及对外加磁场或铁磁接触的依赖。作者旨在开发一种无需磁场、可光谱调控的自旋电流产生方案。

Method: 构建NiPS₃（反铁磁半导体）与WSe₂组成的p-n二极管范德华异质结；通过圆偏振光激发测量光导共振和自旋极化；采用偏振分辨的光电伏特测量（polarization-resolved photogalvanic measurements）表征电流注入机制；结合第一性原理计算分析电场诱导的界面杂化与自旋层锁定（spin-layer locking）效应。

Result: 圆偏振激发产生显著的光导共振，自旋极化率在尼尔温度附近达80%，室温下仍保持30%；部分光谱波段在磁相变后保持极化符号不变；偏振分辨测量确认主导机制为圆注入电流（circular injection-current）；第一性原理计算表明电场引起界面杂化和自旋层锁定，增强光吸收同时保持全局时间反演对称性（time-reversal symmetry）。

Conclusion: 该工作将光谱调谐确立为自旋输运的新控制手段，可在无需磁场和极化翻转的情况下产生自旋电流，确立磁性范德华异质结作为光自旋电子学（opto-spintronics）和光谱可编程自旋器件多功能平台的地位。

Abstract: Efficient generation and control of spin-polarized currents in semiconductors remain central challenges for spin-based electronics, particularly due to impedance mismatch and the reliance on magnetic fields or ferromagnetic contacts. Here, we introduce a materials platform for spectrally programmable spin transport based on a van der Waals heterostructure combining the antiferromagnetic semiconductor NiPS$_3$ with WSe$_2$. In a p-n diode architecture, circularly polarized excitation produces pronounced photoconductive resonances with spin polarization reaching 80% near the Neel temperature and persisting at 30% at room temperature. Remarkably, selected spectral bands retain their polarization sign across the magnetic phase transition, evidencing robust, spectrally protected spin-polarized current generation. Polarization-resolved photogalvanic measurements reveal a dominant circular injection-current mechanism, confirming spin-polarized carrier transport. First-principles calculations show that an applied electric field induces interfacial hybridization and spin-layer locking, giving rise to localized symmetry breaking and enhanced optical absorption while preserving global time-reversal symmetry. These results establish spectral tuning of excitation as a new control knob for spin transport, enabling spin-current generation without magnetic fields or polarization switching. Our findings position magnetic van der Waals heterostructures as a versatile platform for opto-spintronic functionality and spectrally programmable spintronic devices.

</details>


### [114] [Engineering Dirac interface states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24006)
*Gabriele Domaine,Moritz M. Hirschmann,Andreas P. Schnyder*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文发展了一个用于各向异性多谷狄拉克系统中界面态的低能理论，给出了尖锐界面下束缚态存在性、局域化和色散关系的解析表达式，并揭示了切向动力学项对界面态能带平坦化的调控机制，以及平滑界面和动量最高至二次修正的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 各向异性多谷狄拉克系统中界面态的存在性、色散与局域化特性缺乏统一的低能解析描述，且耦合谷间杂化如何受微观界面性质影响尚不明确，需要建立系统的设计原理。

Method: 采用连续模型构建低能理论，利用电流守恒匹配条件求解尖锐界面的束缚态解析解；随后推广至平滑界面，并通过空间平均切向动力学系数修正界面速度；进一步纳入动量二次修正项，最后结合格点模型研究谷间杂化效应。

Result: 界面速度由两侧切向动力学项的加权平均值决定；当两侧切向项彼此抵消时可产生在投影狄拉克点附近平坦到领头阶的界面能带；在反演对称配置下，透明尖锐界面解在所有守恒动量下完全无色散；平滑界面的界面速度由切向系数空间平均修正；动量二次项仅平移界面态能量而不改变线性速度。

Conclusion: 建立了调控狄拉克界面态色散、局域化与谷间杂化的设计原则，并以石墨烯质量畴壁模型为实例展示了色散性同向传播与反向传播的界面态。

Abstract: We develop a low-energy theory of interface states in anisotropic multivalley Dirac systems whose masses and kinetic parameters are allowed to vary across an interface. For sharp interfaces, current-conserving matching conditions yield analytic expressions for the existence, localization and dispersion of the bound states. We show that the interface velocity is determined by the weighted tangential kinetic terms on the two sides of the seam. Their cancellation can suppress the linear velocity and generate an interface band that is flat to leading order near the projected Dirac point. For the special antisymmetric configuration in which both the Dirac mass and the tangential kinetic coefficient reverse sign with unchanged magnitude, the transparent sharp-interface solution is exactly dispersionless for all conserved momenta within the linear Dirac theory, while the surrounding bulk bands remain dispersive. We extend the theory to smooth interfaces, where the modified bound-state envelope changes the linear interface velocity through a spatial average of the tangential kinetic coefficient. We also investigate quadratic corrections in the kinetic \(σ_x\) and \(σ_y\) channels. To first order in their coefficients and through linear order in the interface momentum, these terms shift the interface-state energy but produce no additional correction to the linear velocity. Finally, we combine continuum and lattice models to show how interface modes from distinct valleys hybridize and how the resulting dispersions depend on microscopic interface properties. Our results establish design principles for controlling the dispersion, localization, and hybridization of Dirac interface states. We further examine two graphene-based mass-domain-wall models as experimentally inspired examples of dispersive copropagating and counterpropagating interface states.

</details>


### [115] [Bonding Signatures of Incipient Electron Localization in Topological Chiral Semimetals Near the Metal-Insulator Transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24139)
*Felix Hoff,Jonathan Frank,Mohit Raghuwanshi,Jan Köttgen,Vicky Hasse,Tim Bartsch,Navjot Bamrah,Elias Hildebrand,Carl-Friedrich Schön,Kaustuv Manna,Chandra Shekhar,Claudia Felser,Ricardo P. S. M. Lobo,Matthias Wuttig*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究通过系统性比较拓扑手性半金属（TCSM）与共价化合物、普通金属及金属价固体（metavalent solids）的多维属性指纹，发现TCSM占据独立区域，表现为介于电子局域化与离域化之间的键合状态，并通过原子探针断层扫描、光学测量和超快相干声子谱等手段揭示了其区别于其他材料的独特性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在金属和离子共价绝缘体等传统极限情形之外，电子局域化与离域化如何在固体中竞争尚不清楚。拓扑手性半金属（topological chiral semimetals, TCSM）凭借其独特的晶体对称性，为探索这一问题提供了理想平台。

Method: 作者将TCSM与共价化合物、普通金属和金属价固体（metavalent solids, 即萌芽金属incipient metals）进行系统性比较，构建多维属性指纹（property fingerprint）。采用原子探针断层扫描（atom probe tomography）测量键断裂特征，通过光学性质测量分析带间到带内跃迁的光谱权重转移，并利用超快相干声子谱（ultrafast coherent phonon spectroscopy）研究晶格动力学响应。

Result: TCSM在多维属性指纹中占据区别于其他材料的独立区域。原子探针断层扫描显示其具有异常的键断裂特征，对应介于电子局域化与离域化之间的键合状态。光学测量表明光谱权重从带间跃迁转移到带内跃迁；高电导的TCSM伴随Born有效电荷（Born effective charge）消失，而低电导TCSM仍保持非零值。超快相干声子谱揭示TCSM具有鲁棒的手性B20键合基序，不同于金属价固体中声子驱动的类Peierls不稳定性。

Conclusion: 这些结果从基于性质的键合视角确立了TCSM的独特地位，使其与金属、共价固体和金属价化合物明确区分开来。尽管TCSM与金属价固体都接近金属-绝缘体转变并具有畸变晶体结构，但两者的晶格动力学响应本质不同，表明TCSM代表一种独特的中间键合状态。

Abstract: How do electronic localization and delocalization compete in solids beyond the traditional limiting cases of metals and iono-covalent insulators? Topological chiral semimetals (TCSMs), characterized by their unique crystal symmetry, offer an intriguing platform to explore this question. Here, we systematically compare TCSMs with covalent compounds, ordinary metals, and metavalent solids (incipient metals), and show that TCSMs occupy a distinct region in a multidimensional property fingerprint. Atom probe tomography reveals an unusual bond-rupture signature, consistent with a bonding regime intermediate between electron localization and delocalization. This interpretation is supported by measurements of optical properties showing a transfer of spectral weight from interband to intraband transitions. For highly conductive TCSMs, this transition is accompanied by the disappearance of the Born effective charge, a measure of chemical bond polarizability, while less conductive TCSMs retain a nonzero value. Together, these results identify a property based bonding perspective on TCSMs that distinguishes them from metals, covalent solids, and metavalent compounds. Although metavalent solids and TCSMs both lie near the metal-insulator transition and exhibit distorted crystal structures, ultrafast coherent phonon spectroscopy reveals fundamentally different lattice-dynamical responses: a phonon-driven Peierls-like instability in metavalent solids versus a robust chiral B20 bonding motif in TCSMs.

</details>


### [116] [Classification of Metal - Insulator Transitions: Relating characteristic Properties to Quantum Chemical Bonding Descriptors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24149)
*Tim Bartsch,Carl-Friedrich Schön,Dasol Kim,Raagya Arora,Umesh Waghmare,Matthias Wuttig*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT）计算，根据特征光电子和振动性质的演化将压力诱导的金属-绝缘体转变（metal-insulator transition, MIT）分为三类，并利用一维氢链作为玩具模型揭示了一类被称为"金属价"（metavalent）键合机制，该机制下电子局域化与离域化竞争导致软光学模、Peierls畸变、超导性及低晶格热导率等丰富现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 压力诱导的金属-绝缘体转变在凝聚态物理中具有重要地位，但其背后的键合机制尚缺乏统一的分类和理解。作者希望通过计算分析建立MIT的类型学，并寻找能够刻画不同转变类型背后键合变化的量子化学描述符。

Method: 作者采用密度泛函理论（DFT）计算压力作用下材料的光电子性质与振动性质，依据其演化特征对MIT进行分类。进一步构建一维氢链模型作为玩具模型（toy model），并引入两个量子化学描述符（quantum-chemical descriptors）来量化三类转变背后的键合差异。

Result: 研究识别出三类MIT：离子固体在带隙闭合处连续金属化且声子硬化；共价固体在原子排列和光学声子频率上呈现不连续变化；第三类表现为完全的晶格软化和显著增强的电子-声子耦合（electron-phonon coupling）。一维氢链模型成功复现该行为，并揭示其背后被称为"金属价"（metavalent）的键合机制。两个量子化学描述符能够有效区分三类键合变化。

Conclusion: 在金属价固体中，电子局域化与离域化的竞争导致绝缘侧出现软光学模和Peierls畸变（Peierls distortions），金属侧出现超导性，且在MIT附近呈现低晶格热导率。这一机制为理解压力诱导MIT的多样现象提供了统一的理论框架。

Abstract: Pressure induced metal insulator transitions (MIT) are classified by the evolution of characteristic optoelectronic and vibrational properties calculated with density functional theory. Three classes emerge: ionic solids metallize continuously at band-gap closure with hardening phonons; covalent solids show discontinuous changes in atomic arrangement and optical phonon frequencies; a third class exhibits complete lattice softening and drastically enhanced electron phonon coupling. A one dimensional hydrogen chain reproduces this behavior and serves as a toy model of the underlying bonding mechanism, termed metavalent. Two quantum-chemical descriptors capture the distinct bonding changes behind the three classes. In metavalent solids, competing electron localization and delocalization yield soft optical modes and Peierls distortions on the insulating side, superconductivity on the metallic side, and low lattice thermal conductivity near the MIT.

</details>


### [117] [Depth-Resolved Evolution of Buried Polar Topologies in a PbTiO3/SrTiO3 Superlattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24182)
*Xinxin Hu,Penghan Lu,Noa Varela-Dominguez,Anthony Edgeton,Chang Beom-Eom,Francisco Rivadulla,José Santiso,Yingzhuo Lun,Zhihua Sun,Rafal Dunin-Borkowski,Gustau Catalan,Jordi Arbiol*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过结合深度切片低角度环形暗场扫描透射电子显微镜（LAADF-STEM）、高角度环形暗场STEM（HAADF-STEM）和多切片电子叠层衍射成像（MEP），直接可视化PTO/STO超晶格中的三维原子结构与极化拓扑，揭示了沿束流方向的深度依赖原子位移及极化构型的演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂氧化物中的极性拓扑（polar topologies）由三维原子结构决定，但传统（扫描）透射电子显微镜仅能提供主要投影结构信息，深度灵敏度有限，直接实验测定埋藏的三维极性构型长期以来是重大挑战。

Method: 将深度切片LAADF-STEM、HAADF-STEM与多切片电子叠层衍射成像（MEP）相结合，对PTO/STO超晶格进行深度分辨的原子结构与极化拓扑直接成像。MEP重建同时解析Pb、Ti和O三种亚晶格，实现纳米级深度分辨率的原子位移定量映射。

Result: 深度切片STEM显示明显的焦深依赖衬度变化和Pb原子柱的视在分裂，表明束流方向存在显著结构异质性。MEP重建的三维原子模型揭示了Pb、Ti和O原子中大量深度依赖的位移及极化拓扑的相应演化；涡旋状极化结构在样品表面附近被观察到，但在内部被强烈抑制，并出现截然不同的极化构型。

Conclusion: 常规投影图像中观察到的极化图案可能源于多个深度依赖极性态的叠加，并可能掩盖潜在的三维极化织构。该研究建立了局部原子位移与深度依赖极化拓扑之间的直接实验联系，为复杂氧化物纳米结构中的埋藏功能态研究提供了新机遇。

Abstract: Polar topologies in complex oxides gives rise to a rich spectrum of emergent functionalities and are fundamentally governed by three-dimensional (3D) atomic structures. However, direct experimental determination of buried 3D polar configurations remains a longstanding challenge because conventional (scanning) transmission electron microscopy ((S)TEM) provides primarily projected structural information with limited depth sensitivity. Here, we combine depth-sectioning low-angle annular dark-field (LAADF) STEM, high-angle ADF (HAADF) STEM, and multislice electron ptychography (MEP) to directly visualize the depth-dependent atomic structure and polarization topology in a PTO/STO superlattice. Depth-sectioning STEM reveals pronounced focal-depth-dependent contrast variations and apparent splitting of Pb atomic columns, indicating significant structural heterogeneity along the beam direction. MEP reconstruction simultaneously resolves the Pb, Ti, and O sublattices with nanometer-scale depth resolution, enabling quantitative mapping of atomic displacements throughout the reconstructed volume. The resulting 3D atomic model reveals substantial depth-dependent displacements of Pb, Ti, and O atoms and a corresponding evolution of the polarization topology. Vortex-like polarization structures are observed near the specimen surfaces but become strongly suppressed within the interior, where distinct polarization configurations emerge. These findings show that polarization patterns observed in conventional projection images can arise from the superposition of multiple depth-dependent polar states and may obscure the underlying 3D polarization texture. Our findings establish a direct experimental link between local atomic displacements and depth-dependent polarization topology, opening new opportunities for investigating and engineering buried functional states in complex oxide nanostructures.

</details>


### [118] [Excitonic fingerprints of magnetic configurations and switching in multilayer CrSBr](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24216)
*Lukas Krelle,Ryan Tan,Jakob Conradi,Priyanka Mondal,Wenze Lan,Kseniia Mosina,Regine von Klitzing,Zdenek Sofer,Bernhard Urbaszek*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文研究了磁性半导体 CrSBr 中激子（exciton）与磁有序的耦合，发现体激子和表面激子的能量与振子强度（oscillator strength）可作为不同磁构型及其翻转路径的光谱指纹。通过激子指纹重建了 4 层和 5 层 CrSBr 从铁磁到反铁磁转变过程中的磁构型序列，并揭示了激子对磁序敏感性依赖于其能量位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子态与磁性之间的耦合为实现磁信息的光学读出与控制提供了途径。在磁性半导体 CrSBr 中，激子与磁有序耦合使光学响应对磁化状态高度敏感，但如何利用激子光谱区分不同的磁翻转机制和层依赖磁构型仍待阐明。

Method: 作者系统分析了 CrSBr 中体激子和表面激子的能量与振子强度，将其作为磁构型的指纹特征；通过对比畴壁介导的连续磁化反转与突变的大面积磁化反转，区分不同翻转路径；并利用激子指纹重建 4 层和 5 层 CrSBr 在铁磁向反铁磁转变过程中的连续磁构型。

Result: 研究发现激子对磁序的敏感性高度依赖激子能量：低能激子能够分辨中间态和表面相关构型，而高能激子主要表现为铁磁与反铁磁共振之间振子强度的转移。此外，通过激子指纹成功重建了多层 CrSBr 中磁化翻转路径上的连续磁构型。

Conclusion: 该研究确立了激子光谱学（excitonic spectroscopy）作为层状磁性半导体中层依赖磁构型及其翻转路径的灵敏探测手段，为磁性器件的光学读出应用提供了基础。

Abstract: The coupling between electronic states and magnetism provides a route towards optical readout and control of magnetic information. In the magnetic semiconductor CrSBr, excitons are coupled to magnetic order, making their optical response sensitive to the underlying magnetization. Here, we show that the energy and oscillator strength of bulk and surface excitons provide distinct spectroscopic fingerprints of magnetic configurations and switching pathways. We distinguish domain-wall-mediated magnetization reversal, manifested by continuous spectral evolution as a domain wall traverses the optical spot, from abrupt, large-area magnetization reversal. Using the resulting excitonic fingerprints, we reconstruct successive magnetic configurations in 4- and 5-layer CrSBr during the transition from ferromagnetic to antiferromagnetic order. We further find that the sensitivity to magnetic order is strongly exciton-dependent: low-energy excitons resolve intermediate and surface-related configurations, whereas a higher-energy exciton predominantly exhibits a transfer of oscillator strength between ferromagnetic and antiferromagnetic resonances. These results establish excitonic spectroscopy as a sensitive probe of layer-dependent magnetic configurations and their switching pathways in layered magnetic semiconductors.

</details>


### [119] [Two Microscopic Mechanisms of Piezomagnetism in CoF$_2$ from First-Principles Calculations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24227)
*Hiroshi Katsumoto,Tamio Oguchi,Kunihiko Yamauchi*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 基于第一性原理计算，揭示了金红石结构CoF₂中压磁效应（piezomagnetism）的两种微观机制：在xy剪切应变下，CoF₆八面体局部体积差异导致两个亚晶格磁矩不等从而产生净磁化；在yz剪切应变下，则通过自旋-轨道耦合诱导的Dzyaloshinskii–Moriya相互作用产生自旋倾斜。该双机制特征可能普遍存在于压磁反铁磁体中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CoF₂是典型的压磁材料，近期又作为交变磁体（altermagnet）重新引起关注，其压磁响应虽已从磁对称性角度广泛讨论，但微观起源长期不明，亟需从第一性原理层面阐明。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算方法（first-principles calculations），对不同剪切应变方向（xy和yz）下的CoF₂进行系统研究，分析CoF₆八面体局部体积变化、亚晶格磁矩差异以及自旋倾斜行为。

Result: 发现压磁响应有两种不同机制：xy剪切应变下，两个Co位点周围CoF₆八面体体积差异使两亚晶格磁矩不等，产生净磁化；yz剪切应变下，压磁效应源自Dzyaloshinskii–Moriya相互作用通过自旋-轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）诱导的自旋倾斜（spin canting）。

Conclusion: CoF₂中压磁效应由两种截然不同的微观机制共同驱动，这种双机制并存可能是一般压磁反铁磁体（piezomagnetic antiferromagnets）的普遍特征。

Abstract: Rutile-structured CoF$_2$ has long been recognized as a prototypical piezomagnetic material. Recently, it has attracted renewed interest as an altermagnet, exhibiting spin-split electronic bands even in the absence of spin-orbit coupling. Although the piezomagnetic response of CoF$_2$ has been extensively discussed from the viewpoint of magnetic symmetry, its microscopic origin has remained elusive. First-principles calculations reveal two distinct microscopic mechanisms of piezomagnetism in CoF$_2$. Under $xy$ shear strain, the local volumes of the CoF$_6$ octahedra surrounding the two Co sites become different, leading to unequal magnetic moments on the two sublattices and hence a net magnetization. In contrast, under $yz$ shear strain, the piezomagnetic response originates from spin canting induced by the Dzyaloshinskii--Moriya interaction through spin-orbit coupling. The presence of two distinct microscopic mechanisms may be a general feature of piezomagnetic antiferromagnets.

</details>


### [120] [Influence of Interface Energy Anisotropy on the Solid-state Instability in Ni-based Superalloy: A Multiscale Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24234)
*Sourav Ghosh,Christian Brandl,Rajdip Mukherjee*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新颖的定量多尺度框架，首次将原子尺度的密度泛函理论（DFT）计算的界面能各向异性直接引入介观尺度的相场模拟，用于研究Ni–Al体系中γ'析出相的形态选择与不稳定性，并通过严格解析映射将三维界面各向异性信息降维至二维模拟平面，实现对析出相形貌演化的预测性控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 镍基高温合金的显微结构稳定性关键取决于γ'析出相的形态和演化，而这在原子尺度上受弹性和界面各向异性控制。然而，此前缺乏将原子尺度计算得到的界面能各向异性定量传递到介观相场模拟的系统性方法，导致对析出相形态选择的预测能力不足，亟需建立原子到介观尺度的桥梁。

Method: 首先利用密度泛函理论（DFT）精确预测关键晶面的取向依赖γ/γ'界面能；随后发展严格的解析映射方法，将三维界面各向异性图景系统性地降维至二维模拟平面；最终将DFT信息化的各向异性参数定量地嵌入到同时考虑弹性不均匀性和本征应变的连续相场模型中，开展Ni–Al体系的形态演化模拟。

Result: 模拟结果表明，显式引入基于DFT的界面能各向异性从根本上改变了析出相的形态演化，能够稳健地抑制由过饱和度和弹性效应所促进的不稳定性和刻面化现象，从而揭示了界面能与弹性贡献之间的复杂相互作用。

Conclusion: 该框架成功实现了从原子尺度到介观尺度的建模衔接，为镍基高温合金中析出相形状的预测性控制和定量显微结构设计提供了全新途径，并为先进高温合金的第一性原理引导多尺度模拟奠定了基础。

Abstract: The microstructural stability of nickel-based superalloys critically depends on the morphology and evolution of $γ'$-precipitates, which is governed by elastic and interfacial anisotropies at the atomic scale. Here, we present a novel quantitative multiscale framework that, for the first time, directly incorporates atomistically computed interface energy anisotropy into mesoscale phase-field simulations to elucidate morphological selection and instability in the Ni--Al system. We employ density functional theory (DFT) to accurately predict the orientation-dependent $γ/γ'$ interface energies for key crystallographic planes. A rigorous analytic mapping is then developed to systematically reduce the three-dimensional (3D) interface anisotropy landscape to the two-dimensional (2D) simulation plane. This enables quantitative transfer of DFT-informed anisotropy parameters into a continuum phase-field model that also accounts for elastic inhomogeneity and eigenstrain. Our simulations demonstrate that the explicit inclusion of DFT-based interface energy anisotropy fundamentally alters precipitate morphological evolution, robustly suppressing instability and faceting phenomena otherwise promoted by supersaturation and elastic effects. The framework bridges atomic- to mesoscale modeling, enabling predictive control of precipitate shapes and providing new insights into the interplay of elastic and interfacial contributions in Ni-based superalloys. This approach paves the way for quantitative microstructural design in advanced high-temperature alloys via first-principles-guided multiscale simulation.

</details>


### [121] [Off-stoichiometric variable doping for exceptional power factors in L2$_1$ Fe$_2$VAlM$_x$ (M=Ti, W) epitaxial thin films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24236)
*Jose María Domínguez-Vázquez,Miguel Angel Tenaguillo,Ketan Lohani,Jose J. Plata,Antonio M. Marquez,Olga Caballero-Calero,Alfonso Cebollada,Andrés Conca,Ernst Bauer,Marisol Martín-González*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过溅射共沉积（sputter codeposition）方法，在化学计量比的L2₁ Fe₂VAl薄膜中掺入Ti或W，制备出非化学计量比的薄膜合金。Ti掺杂诱导p型半导体行为，W掺杂使材料偏向n型，从而在同一母体材料体系中同时实现两种载流子类型的调控。所得p型和n型薄膜的最高功率因子（power factor）分别达到1300 μW/m·K²和2100 μW/m·K²，最高热电优值（figure of merit, zT）分别为0.07和0.14。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 化学计量比的Fe₂VAl薄膜热电性能有限，且单一材料体系难以同时满足p型和n型热电材料的需求。通过在单一母体材料中实现两种载流子类型的可控掺杂，可大幅提升热电材料在实际器件中的适用性。

Method: 采用溅射共沉积法在化学计量比Fe₂VAl薄膜中分别掺入Ti或W，形成非化学计量比的薄膜合金。Ti和W部分取代V原子，通过调节取代程度来微调体系的能带结构（band structure）和输运性质（transport properties），实现p型和n型热电性能的同时调控。

Result: Ti掺杂使材料呈现p型半导体行为，W掺杂使材料转向n型。p型和n型薄膜分别获得1300 μW/m·K²和2100 μW/m·K²的最大功率因子，对应的最大zT值分别为0.07和0.14，均优于化学计量比Fe₂VAl薄膜的热电性能。

Conclusion: 通过对Fe₂VAl母体体系进行Ti或W的部分取代掺杂，实现了p型和n型热电性能在同一材料体系中的协同调控，且非化学计量比薄膜的热电性能优于化学计量比薄膜，表明该方法对热电材料的设计具有重要潜力。

Abstract: We show that the addition of Ti or W to stoichiometric L2$_1$ Fe$_2$VAl thin films by sputter codeposition produces off stoichiometric thin film alloys with superior thermoelectric properties than their stoichiometric counterparts. Ti incorporation induces p-type semiconducting behavior, while W incorporation shifts the material toward n-type, hereby enabling simultaneous tuning of both carrier types within a single parent (Fe$_2$VAl) material system, making it highly desirable for thermoelectric devices. The introduction of both Ti and W partly substitutes V in the stoichiometric compound. The partial substitution of V in the stoichiometric alloy allows fine-tuning the band structure of the system and transport properties. With this approach we obtain exceptional maximum power factor values for p and n-type films of 1300 $μ$W/m$\cdot$K$^2$ and 2100 $μ$W/m$\cdot$K$^2$ ,respectively, yielding maximum figures of merit zT of 0.07 and 0.14, respectively.

</details>


### [122] [A hidden low-temperature transformation pathway in compositionally complex materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24357)
*Yujiao Li,Elaheh Akbarnejad,Quentin Bizot,Aleksander Kostka,Natalia Pukhareva,Ridha Zerdoumi,Alan Savan,Matous Mrovec,Ralf Drautz,Baptiste Gault,Dierk Raabe,Alfred Ludwig*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究利用富含缺陷的纳米尺度体积结合原子探针断层扫描（atom-probe tomography）技术，在无需升高温度的情况下，直接观察并重构了亚稳态高熵合金（high entropy alloy）Ag24Au20Pd50Pt6电催化剂中隐藏的相演化路径，揭示富铂相的析出、粗化与再均质化过程及其对析氢催化活性的3.7倍损失影响，从而将隐藏相演化路径确立为材料设计变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数成分复杂材料（CCMs）处于亚稳态，其优异性能往往属于动力学困住（kinetically trapped）状态，但由于扩散控制的原子重新分布过于缓慢，通向低自由能相态的路径在实验可达时间尺度内无法被揭示，形成"盲区"。这一盲区在CCM设计中尤为突出：巨大的成分空间被筛选以追求性能，然而决定性能能否长期维持的低温动力学和相变路径却很少被纳入材料选择考量。

Method: 该方法采用富含缺陷的纳米尺度体积（defect-rich nanoscale volumes）与原子探针断层扫描相结合，在不依赖升高温度加速相变的前提下，直接获取并重构亚稳态Ag24Au20Pd50Pt6电催化剂中隐藏的相演化路径。通过系统改变显微组织初始状态、退火温度和时间来揭示相变过程，并辅以原子模拟（atomistic simulations）独立验证所观察到的富铂相选择。

Result: 实验揭示了面心立方（fcc）基体内富铂相的析出、粗化与再均质化过程；富铂相在均质化后以延迟的动力学可达性再次出现，而延长退火可将相变路径扩展至300°C。原子模拟独立预测了相同的富铂相选择。该相变伴随析氢反应（hydrogen evolution）催化活性的3.7倍损失。

Conclusion: 该研究将隐藏相演化路径确立为材料设计变量：解析这些路径不仅可指导依据合成态性能选择亚稳态CCM，还可考虑其随时间可能达到的相态及相关功能，从而为亚稳态材料的长期稳定性评估提供了新的设计维度。

Abstract: Most compositionally complex materials (CCMs, frequently referred to as high entropy alloys) are metastable and their attractive properties often belong to kinetically trapped states. However, pathways towards lower-free-energy phase states governing long-term stability, can remain hidden because diffusion-controlled atomic redistribution is too slow to be revealed at experimentally accessible timescales. This blind spot is acute in CCM design: enormous compositional spaces are screened for performance, yet the low-temperature kinetics and the associated transformation pathways determining whether that performance persists are rarely considered in material selection. Here we use defect-rich nanoscale volumes coupled with atom-probe tomography to access and reconstruct the hidden phase-evolution pathway in a metastable Ag24Au20Pd50Pt6 electrocatalyst, without relying on elevated temperatures to accelerate the transformation. By varying microstructural starting state, annealing temperature and time, we reveal precipitation of a Pt-rich phase within the fcc matrix, its coarsening and re-homogenization. The Pt-rich phase recurs after homogenization with delayed kinetic accessibility, while prolonged annealing extends the pathway to 300°C. Atomistic simulations independently predict the same Pt-rich phase selection. The transformation is accompanied by a 3.7-fold loss of catalytic activity for hydrogen evolution. These results establish hidden phase-evolution pathways as a materials-design variable: resolving them can guide the selection of metastable CCMs not only for their as-synthesized properties, but also for the phase states and associated functionalities they may access over time.

</details>


### [123] [A colossal dielectric response of Bi1-xSmxFeO3 nanopowders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24420)
*Vladyslav O. Kolupaiev,Olexander S. Pylypchuk,Vladimir N. Poroshin,Denis O. Stetsenko,Ihor V. Fesych,Lesya D. Demchenko,Eugene A. Eliseev,Victor V. Vainberg,Anna N. Morozovska*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究系统测量了Sm掺杂BiFeO3压片粉末样品在20–400°C、100 Hz–100 kHz范围内的介电常数，发现Sm含量显著影响有效介电常数的实部（最高达10^5）和虚部（最高达10^8），其超大弥散峰由弥散铁电-顺电相变与麦克斯韦-瓦格纳-西拉斯（Maxwell-Wagner-Sillars）效应共同作用所致；基于有效介质方法可分离出纳米颗粒本身的巨介电响应，实验趋势与理论模拟结果一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BiFeO3基多铁材料在高温和高频下表现出异常的介电行为，但其巨大介电响应机理尚未完全明确。本研究旨在通过Sm掺杂调控，系统解析扩散性巨介电响应的物理来源，特别是区分本征铁电相变贡献与界面空间电荷效应贡献。

Method: 采用压片粉末样品，在20–400°C温度范围内和100 Hz–100 kHz频率范围内测量Bi1-xSmxFeO3（x=0–0.2）的有效介电常数的实部和虚部；利用有效介质（effective medium）方法对实验数据进行处理，以分离纳米颗粒本体的介电响应；并通过理论模拟对不同相态下介电性质与温度的关系进行对比验证。

Result: Sm掺杂含量显著影响有效介电常数的实部与虚部，二者均在300–400 K温度区间出现展宽的弥散极大峰，实部峰值高达10^5，虚部高达10^8。分析表明该巨介电响应源于弥散铁电-顺电相变与麦克斯韦-瓦格纳-西拉斯效应（由纳米颗粒界面及铁电纳米颗粒-空气界面的空间电荷形成）的复杂相互作用。有效介质方法成功分离出纳米颗粒自身的巨大介电响应，且实验结果与理论模拟的相态-介电性质关联一致。

Conclusion: Bi1-xSmxFeO3纳米颗粒中的巨介电响应并非单一机制所致，而是弥散铁电相变与界面空间电荷极化的协同作用结果；有效介质方法为分离纳米尺度介电贡献提供了有效途径，理论模拟进一步支持了介电行为与纳米颗粒相态之间的对应关系。

Abstract: The dielectric permittivity of the pressed powder samples of Bi1-xSmxFeO3, with Sm content "x" varying in the range 0 - 0.2, has been investigated in the temperature range from 20 to 400 C and the frequency range from 100 Hz to 100 kHz. We have shown that the Sm content impacts significantly the real and imaginary parts of effective dielectric permittivity, which have expanded diffuse maxima with a colossal magnitude up to 105 (for the real part) and up to 108 (for the imaginary one) at temperatures 300 - 400 K. Analysis of experimental data carried has shown that both the real and imaginary parts of effective dielectric permittivity may be comprehensively explained by considering a complex interplay of a diffuse ferroelectric-paraelectric phase transition and the Maxwell-Wagner-Sillars effects, which emerge from the formation of spatial charges at interfaces between nanograins and at the ferroelectric nanoparticle-air interface. Processing of experimental data for the real and imaginary parts of the effective dielectric permittivity within effective medium approach allows us to separate and analyze the colossal dielectric response of the nanoparticles itself. The main trends followed from experiments are supported by the theoretically simulated dependences, which reveal correlations between the temperature behavior of dielectric properties and phase state of the Bi1-xSmxFeO3 nanoparticles.

</details>


### [124] [Plastic Relaxation without Dislocations in $β$-Ga$_2$O$_3$ Heteroepitaxy: A Structural Peculiarity of Ga$_2$O$_3$ Polymorphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24465)
*I. Bertoni,A. Marzegalli,A. Ugolotti,R. He,F. Djurabekova,L. Miglio*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文结合密度泛函理论、连续介质成核理论和分子动力学模拟，揭示了β-Ga₂O₃在失配蓝宝石衬底上生长的无位错塑性弛豫机制——通过共享氧平面下方Ga原子重排，在单原子平面内实现局域α到β弛豫相变，从而完全弛豫β岛状晶粒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验观察到β-Ga₂O₃在失配的c面蓝宝石上生长时薄膜呈弛豫状态，但令人惊讶的是未观测到位错，这表明存在一种非传统的塑性弛豫路径，其潜在机制尚不明确。

Method: 综合运用密度泛函理论（density functional theory）、连续介质成核理论（continuum nucleation theory）和分子动力学模拟（molecular dynamics simulations）三种方法，系统揭示塑性弛豫过程中原子层面的关键机制。

Result: 塑性弛豫通过共享氧平面下方Ga原子重排实现，该氧平面同时属于润湿层与(-201) β-Ga₂O₃结构。这一机制使局域α到β弛豫相变（α to β-relaxed phase transition）在单个原子平面内完成，从而实现β-Ga₂O₃岛的完全弛豫。

Conclusion: 无位错塑性弛豫可在单一原子平面内通过结构相变实现，为理解异质外延中的异常弛豫行为提供了新的物理图景。

Abstract: $β$-Ga$_2$O$_3$ grows as three-dimensional islands on mismatched c-plane sapphire, often on a thin $α$-Ga$_2$O$_3$ wetting layer, and eventually forms a continuous film by island coalescence. Experiments reveal the $β$ film to be relaxed. Surprisingly no dislocations are observed, suggesting an unusual plastic relaxation path. We reveal the key mechanisms underlying this process by combining density functional theory, continuum nucleation theory, and molecular dynamics simulations. Plastic relaxation occurs through rearrangement of the Ga atoms beneath the oxygen plane shared by both the wetting layer and the (-201) $β$-Ga$_2$O$_3$ structures. A local $α$ to $β$-relaxed phase transition is thus realized within a single plane, allowing full relaxation of the $β$-Ga$_2$O$_3$ island.

</details>


### [125] [Structure-Agnostic Prediction of the Electronic Density of States with a Chemical Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24513)
*Ivan D. Rubtsov,Ivan V. Dudakov,Vadim V. Korolev*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出DOSSIER模型，一种基于化学语言模型的电子态密度（DOS）预测方法，可直接从元素组分映射到光谱，无需松弛晶体结构。该模型通过跨模态知识蒸馏预训练，在Mat2Spec基准上达到3.76 states eV⁻¹的平均绝对误差，并能用于筛选高熵合金组分以发现潜在氧还原电催化剂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法计算电子态密度依赖于已松弛的晶体结构，而未被合成或收录的化合物没有现成结构信息，限制了材料筛选的效率。为解决这一瓶颈，本文提出直接由元素组分预测DOS的方法。

Method: 引入DOSSIER，一种化学语言模型（chemical language model），将元素组分直接映射到电子态密度谱。编码器通过从通用机器学习原子间势（universal machine-learning interatomic potential）进行跨模态知识蒸馏（cross-modal knowledge distillation）预训练，并在Mat2Spec基准及扩展的Materials Project数据集上进行评估。

Result: 知识蒸馏在仅有1,000个训练样本时将误差降低11%。在Mat2Spec基准上，DOSSIER的平均绝对误差为3.76 states eV⁻¹，而最优的结构感知模型为3.64 states eV⁻¹。预测光谱可提供具有实用精度的带隙和d带描述符。筛选11,977个二元及13,251个五组分高熵合金组分，成功将与NiPt₃相似d投影DOS的材料排在接近前列，其中包括已知氧还原电催化剂。

Conclusion: DOSSIER证明无需晶体结构的组分到DOS映射具有可行性和实用性，其性能接近结构感知模型，且在无结构先验的大规模材料筛选中展现应用价值。

Abstract: The electronic density of states (DOS) is conventionally computed from a relaxed crystal structure, which is unavailable for compounds that have been neither synthesized nor cataloged. Here we introduce DOSSIER ($\textbf{D}$ensity $\textbf{o}$f $\textbf{S}$tates from $\textbf{S}$to$\textbf{i}$chiometry with $\textbf{E}$ncoder $\textbf{R}$epresentations), a chemical language model that maps elemental composition directly to this spectrum. The encoder is pretrained by cross-modal knowledge distillation from a universal machine-learning interatomic potential; the transfer lowers the error by 11% when only 1,000 training examples are available. On the Mat2Spec benchmark, DOSSIER reaches a mean absolute error of 3.76 states eV$^{-1}$ against 3.64 for the best structure-aware model; on an extended Materials Project dataset, the predicted spectra yield band gaps and $\textit{d}$-band descriptors with useful accuracy. Screening 11,977 binary and 13,251 five-component high-entropy alloy compositions for a $\textit{d}$-projected DOS resembling that of NiPt$_{3}$ places known oxygen reduction electrocatalysts near the top of the ranking.

</details>


### [126] [Interface-Controlled Defect Engineering in TiN/TaN Superlattices for Enhanced Hardness and Fracture Toughness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24607)
*Zecui Gaoa,Qimin Wang,Julian Buchinger,Nikola Koutna,Marcus Hans,Zaoli Zhang,Jochen Schneider,Daniel Primetzhofer,Paul Heinz Mayrhofer*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文探究TiN/TaN超晶格（superlattice）涂层中原子尺度界面化学与缺陷稳定的TaN层如何调控硬度与断裂韧性，通过界面掺杂C、B、Si，发现Si掺杂提升硬度至41 GPa，B掺杂将断裂韧性由2.8提升至4.0 MPam^0.5并保持38 GPa高硬度，确立界面化学为设计陶瓷氮化物超晶格力学性能的额外参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在回答原子尺度界面化学与缺陷稳定的TaN层如何共同决定超晶格涂层的硬度与断裂韧性，并将界面化学确立为陶瓷氮化物超晶格力学性能设计的新维度。

Method: 基于第一性原理（first-principles）预测选择更具损伤容忍性的TaN层，采用反应磁控溅射（reactive magnetron sputtering）合成双周期6 nm的共格超晶格，并分别以C、B、Si进行界面掺杂；结合结构/化学分析、力学测试及第一性原理计算综合表征。

Result: Si优先偏析至界面并进入TiN与TaN两相，C和B主要扩散进入TaN层；未掺杂超晶格硬度34 GPa，Si掺杂升至41 GPa，B掺杂使断裂韧性从2.8升至4.0 MPam^0.5且保持38 GPa高硬度。计算表明空位稳定的TaxNy增强弹性顺应性和弹性对比而非本征韧性，B带来的额外增韧源于界面处局域缺陷辅助能量耗散。

Conclusion: Si最大化界面强化效应，而B在保持较高硬度的同时提供最优的硬度-韧性平衡；界面化学构成调控陶瓷氮化物超晶格力学性能的重要附加设计参数。

Abstract: TiNTaN superlattice coatings were designed to investigate how atomic-scale interface chemistry and defect-stabilized TaN layers govern hardness and fracture toughness. Guided by first-principles predictions identifying TaN-based layers as more damage tolerant than TiN, coherent superlattices with a bilayer period of 6 nm were synthesized by reactive magnetron sputtering and interfacially doped with C, B, or Si. Structural and chemical analyses reveal coherent fcc architectures with well-defined interfaces. Si segregates preferentially to the interfaces while incorporating into both TiN and TaN, whereas C and B predominantly diffuse into the TaN layers, modifying coherency strain, bonding, and defect populations. Consequently, hardness increases from 34 GPa for the undoped superlattice to 41 GPa for the Si-doped architecture, whereas fracture toughness increases from 2.8 to 4.0 MPam0.5 for the B-doped superlattice. First-principles calculations show that vacancy-stabilized TaxNy enhances elastic compliance and elastic contrast rather than intrinsic toughness, while the additional toughening induced by B indicates localized defect-assisted energy dissipation at chemically engineered interfaces. Thus, Si maximizes interface strengthening, whereas B provides the most favourable hardness-toughness balance while preserving high hardness, 38 GPa. These findings establish interface chemistry as an additional design parameter for tailoring the mechanical performance of ceramic nitride superlattices.

</details>


### [127] [Thermal diffuse scattering in TEM: complex absorptive potentials compared to the frozen phonon model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24679)
*Martin Hájek,Ján Rusz*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文比较了透射电子显微镜模拟中处理非弹性散射吸收效应的两种方法：广泛使用的复吸收势方法（complex absorptive potentials method）与基于相关原子运动和爱因斯坦模型（Einstein model）的冻结声子模型（frozen phonon model）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在透射电子显微镜中，电子通过声子激发发生非弹性散射（即热漫散射），需要在弹道传播模型中纳入吸收效应以描述弹性通道的逐步损耗。由于存在多种吸收建模方法，有必要对其进行系统比较。

Method: 本文对两种吸收建模方法进行了对比研究：一种是广泛应用的复吸收势方法，另一种是更为精细的冻结声子模型，后者分别基于相关原子运动与爱因斯坦模型两种原子运动模式进行建模。

Result: 论文对复吸收势方法与冻结声子模型在两种原子运动假设（相关原子运动与爱因斯坦模型）下的表现进行了比较分析，具体结果细节未在摘要中给出。

Conclusion: 摘要未给出最终结论，但从研究设计看，作者旨在揭示不同吸收建模方法之间的一致性、差异及适用范围，为电子显微学模拟中吸收效应的处理提供参考依据。

Abstract: In transmission electron microscopy, electrons undergo inelastic scattering primarily through phonon excitations, known as thermal diffuse scattering. To capture the inelastic scattering effects on the elastic scattering components, absorptive effects must be included in the modeling of electron propagation, accounting for the gradual depletion of the elastic channel of the electron beam. Several approaches to modeling this absorption exist. In this paper, we compare the widely used complex absorptive potentials method to the more elaborate frozen phonon model, based on correlated atomic motion and on the Einstein model of atomic motion.

</details>


### [128] [Multiscale Modelling of Ferroelectrics using a Physics-Informed Neural Network Driven by Molecular Dynamics Data: Parameter Identification and Field Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24733)
*Xuejian Wang,Frank Wendler,Hikaru Auzuma,Michael Zaiser,Shuji Ogata,Ryo Kobayashi,Lei Zeng,Xingchen Tan*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种由分子动力学（molecular dynamics, MD）数据驱动的物理信息神经网络（Physics-Informed Neural Network, PINN）框架，用于建立铁电材料多尺度建模中原子模拟与连续介质相场模型（phase-field model, PFM）之间的桥梁。通过结合监督损失与偏微分方程（PDE）残差损失，该框架既能重建连续尺度下的极化场及相应的应变、应力和能量分布，又能识别相场模型的关键物理参数，并在有限元框架中验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多尺度铁电材料建模中，将原子级模拟与连续介质相场模型相结合仍是一个根本性挑战。核心难点在于如何在连续介质框架内忠实保留离散的原子级信息，同时在介观尺度上准确表征材料行为。

Method: 采用由MD数据驱动的PINN，其损失函数包含两项：监督项用于拟合含畴壁体系的MD模拟得到的离散空间极化分布，物理项则包含稳态PFM偏微分方程的残差。使用自适应梯度归一化（GradNorm）动态调整各损失分量的权重，以保证训练稳定且平衡。通过最小化总损失，模型同时重建连续尺度的极化场、应变、应力及能量分布，并识别相场模型的关键物理参数（特征能量密度、特征长度因子、梯度能量各向异性因子及朗道（Landau）多项式系数）。最后在COMSOL Multiphysics的有限元框架中求解相应PDE，验证所识别参数的有效性。

Result: 使用PINN预测的物理参数在COMSOL Multiphysics有限元框架中求解相应PDE，能够准确再现铁电畴结构及相关的材料响应，包括应力/应变分布和能量分布。

Conclusion: 该框架为建立原子与连续介质描述之间的多尺度联系提供了有效方法，并具有从极化分布直接推断多种材料潜在物理性质的潜力。

Abstract: In multiscale modeling of ferroelectrics, combining atomistic simulation with continuum-scale phase-field models (PFM) remains a fundamental challenge. A key difficulty lies in faithfully capturing discrete atomic-level information within a continuum modeling framework, while accurately representing material behavior at the mesoscale. In this paper, a Physics-Informed Neural Network (PINN) driven by molecular dynamics (MD) data is used. The loss function of the network consists of a supervised term that fits the discrete spatial polarization distributions obtained from MD simulations of systems containing domain walls, and a physics-based term that incorporates the residuals of partial differential equations (PDEs) of steady-state PFM. To ensure stable and balanced training among the different loss components, adaptive gradient normalization (GradNorm) is used to dynamically adjust the task weights. By minimizing the total loss, the model not only reconstructs the polarization field along with the associated strain, stress, and energy landscape at the continuum scale, but also identifies critical physical parameters of the phase-field model, including the characteristic energy density, characteristic length factor, gradient energy anisotropy factor, and Landau polynomial coefficients. By using the PINN-predicted physical parameters in COMSOL Multiphysics to solve the corresponding PDEs within a finite element framework, we demonstrate that these parameters enable accurate reproduction of the ferroelectric domain structure and the associated material response, including stress/strain distributions and energy landscape. This framework provides an effective methodology for establishing multiscale connections between atomistic and continuum descriptions, and holds the potential to infer underlying physical properties directly from polarization distributions for a wide range of materials.

</details>


### [129] [AlV$_2$O$_4$ thin films via in-situ interfacial topotaxy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24821)
*Jeong Rae Kim,Alexis Ashby,Sandra Glotzer,Darryl Shima,Ganesh Balakrishnan,Joseph Falson*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出以 V–O 薄膜作为固态前驱体，通过界面拓扑相变（interfacial topotactic transformation）在 Al₂O₃ (0001) 衬底上外延生长 AlV₂O₄薄膜，该结构中尖晶石框架内同时存在烧绿石型 V².⁵⁺网络与 AlO₄四面体，从而克服传统氧化物外延中因元素氧化条件差异显著而带来的合成困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的氧化物外延（oxide epitaxy）方法在组成元素的氧化条件差异显著时面临挑战，尤其对于具有复杂混合价态（如 V².⁵⁺）的材料体系，热力学与动力学势垒限制了其可合成性（synthesizability）。

Method: 首先在中等温度下生长 V–O 薄膜，随后通过原位超高温后退火（in-situ ultra-high-temperature post-annealing）驱动其与 Al₂O₃ 衬底发生界面拓扑相变反应，从而形成 AlV₂O₄/Al₂O₃ (0001) 外延异质结构。采用透射电子显微镜（transmission electron microscopy）和变温 X 射线衍射（temperature-dependent X-ray diffraction）对结构特性进行表征。

Result: 透射电子显微镜和变温 X 射线衍射分析表明所制备的外延薄膜具有优异的结构特性，并精确复现了已知的电荷有序相变（charge-ordering transition）行为。

Conclusion: 该固态前驱体策略为在热力学和动力学势垒限制常规合成的条件下，实现具有复杂氧化态的外延结构提供了一种新颖的合成路径。

Abstract: Conventional oxide epitaxy approaches face challenges when the oxidation conditions of constituent elements differ significantly. Here we demonstrate that V--O thin films can serve as solid-phase precursors for epitaxial AlV$_2$O$_4$, comprising a pyrochlore V$^{2.5+}$ network coexisting with AlO$_4$ tetrahedra within the spinel structure. The epitaxial AlV$_2$O$_4$/Al$_2$O$_3$ (0001) heterostructures are realized via interfacial topotactic transformation, involving V--O growth at a moderate temperature followed by in-situ ultra-high-temperature post-annealing to drive a reaction with the Al$_2$O$_3$ substrate. Transmission electron microscopy and temperature-dependent X-ray diffraction analyses reveal excellent structural characteristics that closely reproduce the known charge-ordering transition. This study presents a novel approach to realizing epitaxial structures with convoluted oxidation states where thermodynamic and kinetic barriers would otherwise limit synthesizability.

</details>


### [130] [Predicting THz Generation Capability of Organic Crystals through Data Mining and Crystal Nonlinearity Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24835)
*Sin Hang Ho,Ashton Roma,Connor Barlow,Matthew Lutz,Natalie Green,Stacey Smith,David Michaelis,Jeremy A Johnson*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文利用密度泛函理论（DFT）计算与数学模型，基于晶体结构预测有机材料的非线性介电极化（P^NL）和非线性极化率系数（χ^(2)_IJK），并在剑桥结构数据库中筛选出的单组分晶体、共晶体和离子晶体上进行了验证，实验太赫兹（THz）产生效率与计算结果一致。研究发现多个候选晶体在P^NL值上接近或超过DAST、OH1和BNA等先进THz产生晶体，并在对比简化模型后指出综合性方法应作为评估单晶材料非线性光学性质的标准方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性光学（nonlinear optical, NLO）材料在太赫兹产生等应用中具有关键作用，但已有评估手段常用简化假设，缺乏基于完整晶体结构的系统性预测方法。作者旨在建立一种既可预测又可验证的计算框架，以更准确地评估有机晶体的二阶非线性光学性能。

Method: 作者采用DFT计算结合数学模型，从晶体结构出发预测非线性介电极化P^NL和非线性极化率张量分量χ^(2)_IJK。研究样本来自对剑桥结构数据库（Cambridge Structural Database）的数据挖掘，涵盖单组分晶体、共晶体（co-crystals）和离子晶体（ionic crystals）三类，并将计算结果与已知THz发生器的实验THz产生效率进行对照验证。

Result: 计算值与已知THz发生器的实验效率具有一致性；数据挖掘得到的多个候选晶体其P^NL值与DAST、OH1和BNA等先进晶体相当或更大，显示出在NLO应用中的潜力。此外，作者对比了完整张量模型与常用非线性简化模型，发现简化模型存在不足，综合性计算方法优于传统简化处理。

Conclusion: 研究表明，基于DFT和完整张量模型的综合计算方法应作为评估单晶材料非线性光学性能的标准方法，而非传统简化近似。该方法能够有效筛选和验证具有优异NLO潜力的有机晶体材料。

Abstract: We report the use of DFT computation and mathematical models to predict the nonlinear dielectric polarization (P^{NL}) and nonlinear susceptibility coefficients (χ^{(2)}_{IJK}) of organic materials based on their crystal structures. We apply this computation approach on single-component crystals, co-crystals and ionic crystals found through data mining the Cambridge Structural Database. We verify these computational results with experimental terahertz (THz) generation efficiencies for known THz generators, demonstrating consistency between the measurements and the computed values. Several mined structures show similar or larger PNL values compared to state-of-the-art THz generation crystals DAST, OH1 and BNA, suggesting great potential for their use in nonlinear optical (NLO) applications. Importantly, we also compared the resulting model of nonlinear optical tensor components with commonly-used simplifications of nonlinearity, showing that the comprehensive approach should be the standard method to evaluate the nonlinear optical properties of single-crystalline materials.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [131] [TRACE: Transition-Aware Residual Control for Multi-Objective Materials Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23631)
*Kang Zhou,Yujia Tong,Yong Tao,Jingling Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TRACE（transition-aware residual control framework），一个面向多目标材料发现（multi-objective materials discovery）的转换感知残差控制框架。TRACE将已执行的编辑（edit）作为反馈的基本单元，记录父-子编辑转换及对应属性变化，聚合转换证据以估计可复用的编辑效应，并据此排序未来编辑候选。在受控同骨干对比实验中，TRACE将宏平均命中率（macro-average hit rate）从基线LLEMA的18.13%提升至25.96%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体（LLM agents）在多目标材料发现中主要存储评估过的候选材料及其分数，因而知道哪些材料成功，却不清楚哪些可执行的编辑（executable edits）引发了有用的属性变化。当目标相互冲突且修改一个属性可能损害另一属性时，缺乏编辑级反馈使得局部优化（local refinement）变得困难。

Method: TRACE框架以已评估的编辑为基本反馈单元，具体包括三个步骤：(1) 将每次局部优化记录为"父编辑-子编辑-转换"（parent-edit-child transition）三元组并附上观测到的属性增量（property deltas）；(2) 聚合转换证据以估计可复用的编辑效应（reusable edit effects）；(3) 根据编辑预测减少当前候选剩余约束违反（constraint violations）同时避免损害已满足目标的能力，对未来编辑进行排序。

Result: 在受控同骨干（same-backbone）对比实验中，TRACE相较当前最先进的LLM智能体基线LLEMA，将宏平均命中率从18.13%提升至25.96%。

Conclusion: TRACE通过将编辑作为反馈单元，使每次昂贵的属性评估（property evaluation）能更有效地指导后续搜索步骤，显著提升多目标材料发现中LLM智能体的整体性能。

Abstract: Multi-objective materials discovery with LLM agents is often limited not only by how many candidates can be proposed, but by how effectively each costly property evaluation informs the next search step. Existing agents mainly store evaluated candidates and their scores, so they know which materials succeeded but not which executable edits caused useful property changes. This makes local refinement difficult when objectives compete and an edit that improves one property may damage another. We propose TRACE, a transition-aware residual control framework that treats evaluated edits as the basic unit of feedback. TRACE records each local refinement as a parent-edit-child transition with observed property deltas, aggregates transition evidence to estimate reusable edit effects, and ranks future edits by their predicted ability to reduce the current candidate's remaining constraint violations while avoiding damage to already satisfied objectives. In a controlled same-backbone comparison, TRACE improves over LLEMA, the state-of-the-art LLM-agent baseline, raising macro-average hit rate from 18.13\% to 25.96\%.

</details>

### [132] [Compositionality in quantum reference frame perspectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23698)
*Bruna Sahdo,Esteban Castro-Ruiz*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文深入研究量子参考系（quantum reference frames, QRFs）视角下子系统如何组合的问题。基于Castro-Ruiz与Oreshkov于2025年提出的QRF形式体系，文章建立了QRF视角的一致性层级结构，定义了子系统的添加与移除过程，并通过"额外粒子"自由度避免了"第三粒子悖论"。研究表明，在QRF视角下组合子系统不同于并推广了标准量子理论情形，并非所有态都能以张量积形式附加，文章还刻画了兼容态的集合，并引入"经典化"（classicalisation）程序从一般QRF中恢复类经典视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解复合系统通过其组成部分进行分解是物理学的基本实践，然而在量子参考系（quantum reference frames, QRFs）语境下，将通常的量子理论组合概念与QRF视角结合会引发微妙问题，例如"第三粒子悖论"（paradox of the third particle）。本文旨在深入探究如何在QRF视角下正确组合子系统，以解决这一悖论。

Method: 本文基于Castro-Ruiz与Oreshkov（2025年）最近提出的QRF形式体系展开研究。首先展示如何将外部观察者的参考系内化（internalise）并在该框架内处理，从而建立一致的QRF视角层级结构，用于定义子系统的添加与移除。随后利用所谓"额外粒子"（extra-particle）自由度来给出子系统的自洽处理，并刻画可兼容的态集合。最后引入"经典化"（classicalisation）程序，从一般QRF中恢复类经典视角。

Result: 研究表明，由于"额外粒子"自由度的存在，该形式体系能够对子系统提供自洽处理，从而从构造上避免任何悖论。一致性进一步意味着在QRF视角下组合子系统不同于并推广了标准量子理论情形：并非每个态都能以张量积形式相对于QRF附加，文章刻画了兼容态的集合。此外，经典化程序能够规避上述态限制，但承载不同的操作含义，这一差异通过具体例子加以阐明。

Conclusion: 本文确立了QRF视角下子系统组合的一致框架，推广了标准量子理论中的组合概念，并通过经典化程序提供了一条恢复类经典视角的途径，尽管其操作含义有所不同。该工作为量子参考系理论中组合问题的自洽处理提供了系统性的形式化方案。

Abstract: Understanding a composite system through its constituents is a fundamental practice in physics. In the context of quantum reference frames (QRFs), however, combining the usual quantum-theoretic notion of composition with QRF perspectives gives rise to subtle issues, such as the 'paradox of the third particle'. Here we study in depth how to compose subsystems in QRF perspectives, building on a recent formalism for QRFs [E.Castro-Ruiz and O.Oreshkov, 2025]. We first show how the frames of external observers can be internalised and treated within the framework. This establishes a consistent hierarchy of QRF perspectives, which we use to define the adding and removing of subsystems. We then explain how, owing to the so-called 'extra-particle' degrees of freedom, the formalism gives a consistent treatment of subsystems, avoiding any paradoxes by construction. Consistency then implies that composing subsystems in a QRF perspective differs from, and generalises, the standard quantum-theoretic case. In particular, not every state can be appended in tensor-product form relative to a QRF, and we characterise the set of compatible states. Finally, we introduce 'classicalisation', a procedure that recovers a classical-like perspective from a general QRF. This procedure circumvents the aforementioned state restrictions but carries a different operational meaning, which we study through a concrete example.

</details>


### [133] [Time-Dependent Tunneling in the Thin-Barrier Limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24032)
*Tanmay Vachaspati,Frank Wilczek,Zara Yu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对量子隧穿（quantum tunneling）中作用量（action）较小的情形展开分析，与通常WKB分析适用的作用量较大情形形成对照。作者以势垒面积倒数作为控制参数发展微扰方法，并在1+1维下对多个例子进行了研究。研究发现，谐振情形下束缚粒子的隧穿概率随时间按∝t²增长，非谐振情形下则随时间线性增长；文章同时给出了准束缚态粒子逃逸至连续态的时变隧穿波函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的WKB分析仅适用于隧穿作用量较大（即势垒又高又宽）的情形，但现实中还存在作用量较小的情形，例如穿过又高又薄势垒的隧穿，此类情形缺少系统的分析方法。

Method: 作者发展了一种微扰分析方法，以势垒下方面积的倒数作为控制参数（control parameter），并将该方法应用于1+1维中的多个具体例子。

Result: 在谐振情形下，束缚粒子的隧穿概率随时间呈∝t²增长；在非谐振情形下，隧穿概率随时间线性增长。此外，文章不仅计算出隧穿概率，还给出了粒子从准束缚态（quasi-bound state）逃逸至连续态（continuum）的时变隧穿波函数。

Conclusion: 本文为小作用量量子隧穿问题提供了一种新的微扰分析方法，揭示出隧穿概率的时间增长行为依赖于谐振条件，并给出了时间依赖的隧穿波函数的完整描述。

Abstract: The usual WKB analysis for quantum tunneling applies when the tunneling action is large, as it is for tall, wide potential barriers. In contrast we analyze tunneling when the action is small, as it is for tunneling across a tall, thin barrier. We develop a perturbative analysis where the control parameter is the inverse of the area under the potential barrier and apply our technique to several examples in $1+1$ dimensions. In resonant situations for bound particles we find that the tunneling probability grows with time as $\propto t^2$, while in non-resonant situations it grows linearly with time. We evaluate not only the tunneling probability but also the time-dependent tunneling wavefunction for a particle that escapes to infinity, {\it i.e.} from a quasi-bound state to the continuum.

</details>


### [134] [Tunable-Size Unruh-DeWitt Detector in a Multimode Cavity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24451)
*Simon Brunner,Farokh Mivehvar,Helmut Ritsch,Arkadiusz Kosior*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Bose-Einstein凝聚体的动量选择性Unruh-DeWitt探测器模型，利用光腔介导的俘获原子与凝聚体耦合，通过腔模结构控制的有效尺寸过滤非声子激发，从而在实验可及的加速度范围内增强相对论类比信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于Bose-Einstein凝聚体（BECs）的量子模拟器是研究实验室中相对论性量子场现象的有力平台，可模拟相对论类比粒子探测器。然而，此类探测器通常耦合到超出声学声子（acoustic phonon）区的激发，非线性Bogoliubov色散带来的修正会使有效相对论描述失效。

Method: 提出一种内在动量选择性的Unruh-DeWitt探测器模型，其中光镊（tweezer）俘获的原子通过多模光学腔耦合到凝聚体。腔介导的相互作用核使得点状原子能够对凝聚体中可控有限区域内的密度涨落进行采样，等效于有限尺寸探测器。有效探测器尺寸可通过腔模结构调节，从而确定可控的动量截断（momentum cutoff），滤除非声子激发。

Result: 解析计算了均匀线加速度和匀速圆周运动下该类探测器的响应，表明动量选择性能够在实验可及加速度下增强信号。该方案与现有腔量子电动力学（cavity-QED）平台兼容。

Conclusion: 该方案为类比量子场测量或相对论量子信息协议提供了通用设置，克服了传统类比探测器受非线性色散修正限制的局限。

Abstract: Quantum simulators based on Bose-Einstein condensates (BECs) provide a powerful platform to study relativistic quantum-field phenomena in the lab and in particular emulate relativistic analog particle detectors. However, such detectors typically couple to excitations beyond the acoustic phonon regime, so that the nonlinear Bogoliubov dispersion introduces corrections that cause the effective relativistic description to break down. We propose to overcome this limitation using an intrinsically momentum-selective Unruh-DeWitt detector model in which a tweezer-trapped atom couples to the condensate through a multimode optical cavity. Although the atom is effectively pointlike, the cavity-mediated interaction kernel causes it to sample density fluctuations over a controllable finite region of the BEC equivalent to a finite-size detector. The effective detector size is tunable via the cavity mode structure determining a controllable momentum cutoff that filters out nonphononic excitations. We analytically calculate the response of such a detector for uniform linear acceleration and uniform circular motion and show that this momentum selectivity can enhance the signal at experimentally accessible accelerations. Compatible with existing cavity-QED platforms our scheme thus provides a versatile setting for analog quantum-field measurements or relativistic quantum-information protocols.

</details>


### [135] [Certified Fidelity Susceptibility from Classical Shadows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24732)
*Kiran Adhikari*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用随机单副本测量验证保真度磁化率（fidelity susceptibility）的方法，该方法结合 Krylov 子空间方法与基于预解式的重整化表述，在重复基态制备过程中获得精确保真度磁化率的单调下界，并具有几何收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 保真度磁化率是研究量子相变（quantum phase transitions）和量子计量学（quantum metrology）的有力探针，但在量子设备上直接评估其值具有挑战性，因此需要可验证的替代方案。

Method: 将 Krylov 子空间方法与基于预解式（resolvent-based）的重整化表述相结合，利用跨重复基态制备的随机单副本测量来认证保真度磁化率。

Result: 所得到的近似值构成精确保真度磁化率的单调下界，并呈几何级数收敛（geometric convergence）。文中还给出了在计量学与线性静态磁化率方面的应用。

Conclusion: 该方法为在量子设备上认证保真度磁化率提供了可行的途径，并在量子计量学和线性静态磁化率领域具有潜在应用价值。

Abstract: Fidelity susceptibility is a useful probe of quantum phase transitions and quantum metrology, but its direct evaluation on a quantum device is challenging. By integrating Krylov-subspace methods with a resolvent-based reformulation, we present a method for certifying fidelity susceptibility using randomized single-copy measurements across repeated ground-state preparations. The resulting approximations form monotone lower bounds to the exact fidelity susceptibility and converge geometrically. We also present applications to metrology and linear static susceptibilities.

</details>


### [136] [Hamiltonian Learning at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23707)
*Nathan Johnson,Eugene Dumitrescu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用张量网络（tensor network）技术在大规模带噪声条件下模拟哈密顿量学习（Hamiltonian learning）流程，基于矩阵乘积算子构造近似稳态，给出了学习估计误差的解析界，并通过数值模拟在N=300格点的混合场Ising模型自旋链上验证了学习误差对两类噪声的稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习量子系统的哈密顿量对于理解与控制其动力学至关重要，但实际实验中不可避免存在误差，因此需要深入理解学习协议在噪声条件下的容错能力与稳定性。

Method: 采用基于近似稳态的哈密顿量学习技术，近似稳态通过矩阵乘积工具（matrix product tools）构造；利用张量网络技术在带噪声的大规模系统中仿真学习流程，并提供学习估计误差的解析上界。

Result: 解析地给出了哈密顿量学习估计误差的界，数值模拟表明学习误差在两类误差族下具有稳定性；该流程可扩展学习N=300格点自旋链的混合场Ising模型哈密顿量。

Conclusion: 通过大规模仿真揭示了误差对哈密顿量学习过程影响的实际局限性，为未来实验设计提供了有价值的经验，并展望了可支持哈密顿量学习实验的后续研究方向。

Abstract: Learning a quantum system's Hamiltonian is crucial for understanding and controlling its dynamics and has recently become a topic of widespread interest. To understand the learning protocol's error tolerances, i.e. its stability in the presence of inevitable errors, this work utilizes tensor network techniques to emulate Hamiltonian learning workflows at scale and with noise. Specifically, we employed a Hamiltonian learning technique based on approximate stationary states which are constructed using matrix product tools. We provide analytic bounds on the Hamiltonian learning estimation errors and perform numerical simulations that highlight learning error's stability under two families of errors. Using our workflow, we are able to scale up the protocol and learn mixed-field Ising model Hamiltonians of an $N=300$ site spin chain. By simulating the protocol at large scale, and empirically studying its practical limitations, our analysis of how errors affect the Hamiltonian learning process provides valuable lessons for future experiments. We conclude by discussing the avenues our work opens as well as future work that can support Hamiltonian learning experiments.

</details>


### [137] [Towards Stirling cooler operable single-photon sources based on low-noise GaAs quantum dots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24409)
*Maximilian Aigner,Jana Schlücking,Eva Schöll,Christian Weidinger,Gabriel Undeutsch,Ievgen Brytavskyi,Thomas Oberleitner,Tobias Maria Krieger,Ailton Jose Garcia Junior,Melina Peter,Thomas K. Bracht,Michał Gawełczyk,Saimon Filipe Covre da Silva,Santanu Manna,Yusuf Karli,Gregor Weihs,Doris E. Reiter,Armando Rastelli*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了低温至升温条件下GaAs量子点负三子（negative trion）辐射衰变产生光子的双光子干涉特性，实验测量与考虑载流子-声子相互作用及激发态耦合的理论计算定量吻合。实验发现温度从最低值升至55 K时，光子不可区分度（indistinguishability）从0.966(6)急剧下降至0.05(4)，并通过腔增强（Purcell增强）手段在32 K下将不可区分度从0.314(25)提升至0.80(3)，为该温度下已知的最高报道值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光子量子技术应用需要产生不可区分度接近1的光子源，且理想情况下不依赖苛刻的低温冷却系统。当前缺乏对温度升高时光子不可区分度退化机制的定量理解，需要探索在较高温度下维持高不可区分度的可行途径。

Method: 在低噪声GaAs量子点中对负三子辐射衰变产生的光子开展温度依赖的双光子干涉（two-photon interference）测量，并将实验结果与包含载流子-声子相互作用及激发态耦合的理论模型进行定量对比；随后通过腔增强发射（cavity-enhanced emission）验证利用Purcell增强提高高温下光子不可区分度的策略。

Result: 在最低温度下，发射线宽仅比傅里叶极限（Fourier limit）宽6(2)%，光子不可区分度达0.966(6)；而在55 K时不可区分度骤降至0.05(4)。这一退化可归因于与能量接近的激发三子态的耦合。通过腔增强发射，32 K下光子不可区分度从0.314(25)提升至0.80(3)，为同类温度下的最高报道值。

Conclusion: 温度升高导致的光子不可区分度退化可由激发态耦合机制定量解释；采用Purcell增强发射速率或增大激发态能量间隔可有效缓解该退化，为开发无需深低温冷却的高性能光子源提供了实验验证和理论指导。

Abstract: For photonic quantum technology applications, sources capable of emitting photons with indistinguishability close to unity are essential. Ideally, these sources should not require demanding cooling systems. Here, we present temperature-dependent two-photon-interference measurements on photons produced by the radiative decay of the negative trion in a low-noise GaAs quantum dot, which are in quantitative agreement with theoretical calculations accounting for carrier-phonon interactions and coupling to excited states. While at at the lowest explored temperatures the emission linewidth reaches values only 6(2) % above the Fourier limit and the indistinguishability I between subsequently emitted photons reaches 0.966(6), the latter drops to 0.05(4) at 55 K. We show that this loss can be explained with the coupling with energetically close excited trion states and suggest that the photon indistinguishability at elevated temperatures can be increased by employing Purcell enhancement of the emission rate or by increasing the energy separation of the excited states. Using cavity-enhanced emission, we experimentally verify the first route and demonstrate an improvement in photon indistinguishability from 0.314(25) to 0.80(3) at 32 K, which - to our knowledge - is the highest reported value at such temperature.

</details>

### [138] [Imaging atomic scale stochasticity: noise in the nearly commensurate charge density wave of 1T-TaS$_2$ for leaky-integrator-and-fire neuromorphic emulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24693)
*Michael Verhage*

Main category: physics.app-ph

TL;DR: 本研究利用扫描隧道显微镜（STM）作为电荷密度波（CDW）相关性的高局域探针，在室温下研究了1T-TaS₂中的原子尺度CDW噪声。通过统计分析和介观模型，揭示了CDW失配畴壁的分形结构与重组过程与时间相关电流突发的关联，该噪声行为模拟了泄漏积分放电（leaky-integrate-and-fire）神经元的动力学，表明隧道结中的原子尺度电流突发可作为尖峰神经模拟器中的有效非线性元件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低维材料有望用于模拟神经元样放电行为，理解其原子尺度的起源机制有助于将其集成到神经形态硬件（neuromorphic hardware）中，因此需要深入探究该行为的微观来源。

Method: 利用扫描隧道显微镜（STM）作为高度局域的探针，在室温下对1T-TaS₂中电荷密度波（CDW）的原子尺度噪声进行测量；对隧道结噪声进行统计分析，并引入介观模型来解释时间相关的电流突发，该模型将CDW失配畴壁的分形结构与重组过程相联系。

Result: 结果表明，室温下隧道结中的原子尺度电流突发可以充当尖峰神经模拟器中的有效非线性元件，通过丰富的CDW动力学实现仿生信息处理，并支持尖峰器件的微型化。

Conclusion: 该噪声行为能够模拟泄漏积分放电神经元动力学，展示了CDW系统在室温下用于生物启发式处理（bioinspired processing）的潜力，为尖峰器件的微型化提供了支持。

Abstract: Low-dimensional materials are promising materials for emulating neuron-like firing were understanding of the atomic-scale origin of this behavior can benefit integration into neurormophic hardware. Using scanning tunneling microscopy (STM) as a highly local probe of correlations in the charge density wave (CDW), we study atomic-scale CDW noise in $1T\text{-}\mathrm{TaS}_2$ at room temperature. We statistically analyze the tunnel-junction noise and, to explain the temporally correlated current bursts, introduce a mesoscopic model in which the fractal structure and reorganization of CDW discommensurations are related. This noise mimics leaky-integrate-and-fire neuron dynamics. Our results show that atomic-scale current bursts in the tunnel junction can act as effective nonlinear elements in spiking neural emulators, enabling bioinspired processing through rich CDW dynamics at room temperature and supporting the miniaturization of spiking devices.

</details>

### [139] [On Split Forms of Fusion Categories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23743)
*César Galindo*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文通过框架等价（framed equivalences）将融合范畴（fusion categories）的分裂伽罗华下降（split Galois descent）形式化，将其归约为群论分裂问题，证明了量子群在单位根处的融合范畴在最大全实子域上存在分裂实形式，并分析了标准编织（braiding）及各类变形（zesting）对实下降的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 融合范畴在单位根量子群上的实下降问题（real descent）此前缺乏统一的范畴论框架，且标准编织与融合结构对分裂实形式存在性的影响尚不明确。本文旨在提供一个系统性的框架等价方法，将存在性判定归约为群论问题，并厘清编织与变形操作对实结构的约束。

Method: 作者通过框架等价引入分裂伽罗华下降的范畴论表述，将分裂形式的存在性转化为群论分裂问题，并将关联的二次障碍（degree-two obstruction）分解为范畴相干性与简单对象（simple objects）的稳定下降两部分。对于单位根处的量子群，利用拟R矩阵（quasi-R-matrix）使对合（bar involution）保持张量相容性，进而证明融合范畴在最大全实子域上的分裂形式存在性。此外，对关联变形（associative zesting）与编织变形（braided zesting）分别分析其对实下降的保持或破坏效应，并通过上同调和Frobenius–Schur指标给出点态范畴与有限群表示范畴的实下降判据。

Result: 作者证明了与单连通根数据（simply connected root datum）关联的融合范畴\mathcal C(\mathfrak g,k)在最大全实子域上存在分裂形式，且其所有分圆伽罗华共轭亦满足此性质；特别地，每个\mathcal C(\mathfrak g,k)均具有分裂实形式。然而，标准编织下的分裂实形式仅存在于\mathcal C(E_8,1)和\mathcal C(D_{4m},1)（m \geq 1）中。关联变形会破坏\mathcal C(\mathfrak g,k)的实下降，而编织变形仍保留其底层融合范畴的分裂实形式。

Conclusion: 本文建立的分裂伽罗华下降框架统一了融合范畴实结构的存在性判定，揭示了编织结构对实形式的强限制，并表明变形操作对实下降的影响取决于其类型。所得结果对融合范畴的分类理论及量子群表示范畴的算术性质具有直接意义。

Abstract: We formulate split Galois descent for fusion categories through framed equivalences, reducing the existence of a split form to a group-theoretic splitting problem. The associated degree-two obstruction combines categorical coherence with stable descent of the simple objects, while fusion spaces impose index and parity restrictions. For quantum groups at roots of unity, we use the quasi-$R$-matrix to make the bar involution tensor-compatible and prove that the underlying fusion category $\mathcal C(\mathfrak g,k)$, associated with the simply connected root datum, has a split form over the maximal totally real subfield of the cyclotomic field generated by the quantum parameter. The same holds for all its cyclotomic Galois conjugates; in particular, every $\mathcal C(\mathfrak g,k)$ has a split real form. By contrast, no split real form exists for the standard braiding except on $\mathcal C(E_8,1)$ and $\mathcal C(D_{4m},1)$, $m \geq 1$. We further show that associative zesting can destroy real descent of $\mathcal C(\mathfrak g,k)$, while every braided zesting of $\mathcal C(\mathfrak g,k)$ still admits a split real form as an underlying fusion category. Applications to pointed and finite-group representation categories give criteria for real descent in terms of cohomology and Frobenius--Schur indicators.

</details>


### [140] [Bianchi identities in noncommutative geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24536)
*Paolo Aschieri*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种非交换代数（noncommutative algebra）上微分几何的新构造框架，并基于Cartan演算建立了任意联络（connections）的Bianchi恒等式的全局等价形式，获得了广义相对论中第一、第二Bianchi恒等式的非交换类比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在将经典微分几何中的Bianchi恒等式推广到非交换几何（noncommutative geometry）框架下，特别是针对承载三角Hopf代数（triangular Hopf algebra）表示的非交换代数，并处理不满足双模联络（bimodule connection）条件的任意联络，以填补已有理论在这一方向上的空白。

Method: 首先，论文引入了携带三角Hopf代数表示的非交换代数的微分几何基本框架，其非对易性由通用R矩阵（universal R-matrix）编码，并由此规范地构造微分几何结构。随后，论文提出一种新的方法来推导任意联络的曲率（curvature）与挠率（torsion）所满足的Bianchi恒等式，该方法利用联络的Cartan演算（Cartan calculus），证明了以外微分形式（exterior forms）和张量场（tensor fields）表述的Bianchi恒等式在全局形式上是等价的。

Result: 论文成功获得了非交换版本的广义相对论中第一与第二Bianchi恒等式，并证明了联络的Bianchi恒等式在两种数学表述（外形式与张量场）下的全局等价性，且该推导适用于一般（非双模）联络。

Conclusion: 该工作将经典微分几何中Bianchi恒等式的理论推广至非交换框架，特别是通过通用的微分几何构造，为非交换代数上的任意联络提供了完整的数学描述，为非交换广义相对论等后续研究提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We first present an introduction to the differential geometry of noncommutative algebras that carry a representation of a triangular Hopf algebra. Their noncommutativity is encoded in the universal R-matrix. The differential geometry is canonically constructed from these data. We then develop a new approach to the Bianchi identities for curvature and torsion of arbitrary connections (not necessarily bimodule connections). Using the Cartan calculus for connections, we prove the equivalence of their global formulations in terms of exterior forms and tensor fields. In particular, we obtain the noncommutative analogues of the first and second Bianchi identities familiar from general relativity.

</details>

### [141] [Intrinsic Structure of Elasticity in Hilbert Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24264)
*Cristian G. Gebhardt,Johannes Lankeit,Marc C. Steinbach*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文利用泛函分析（functional analysis）的标准工具，揭示了弹性力学静力平衡问题中四个正交分解（orthogonal decomposition）的Hilbert空间结构，该结构在任意空间维度、任意边界条件以及经典与数据驱动（data-driven）表述下均普遍成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数学弹性力学历史悠久且文献庞大，但其核心蕴含的丰富而清晰的结构却很少以显式且统一的形式呈现。作者希望揭示这一内在结构，并阐明其在经典表述和数据驱动连续介质力学（data-driven continuum mechanics）中保持不变的本质属性。

Method: 作者使用泛函分析的标准工具，系统性地构造了刻画弹性体小变形静力平衡的Hilbert空间的四个正交分解，并验证其在任意空间维度、任意边界条件、以及经典与本构关系被材料数据集替代的数据驱动表述下均成立。

Result: 文章展示了四个Hilbert空间正交分解，普遍适用于任意空间维度与任意边界条件，同时在经典框架与数据驱动框架中保持一致；特别指出当本构定律（constitutive laws）被材料数据集（material data sets）取代时，该内在结构保持不变。

Conclusion: 弹性力学核心存在一个显式、统一且结构透明的数学表征，该表征不仅刻画了经典弹性静力平衡，也为数据驱动连续介质力学提供了不变的内在结构框架。

Abstract: Mathematical elasticity has a long history and a huge body of literature. Surprisingly, at its heart elasticity exhibits a rich and transparent structure that, to our knowledge, is rarely presented in an explicit and unified form. Using standard tools from functional analysis, this article reveals four orthogonal decompositions of Hilbert spaces that characterize the static equilibrium of an elastic body at small deformations, universally for arbitrary spatial dimension, arbitrary boundary conditions, and classical as well as data-driven formulations. Regarding data-driven continuum mechanics, it highlights the intrinsic structure that remains unchanged when constitutive laws are replaced by material data sets.

</details>


### [142] [On a bulk-surface Navier-Stokes-Cahn-Hilliard model: Existence of weak solutions and asymptotic limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24647)
*Jonas Stange*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个热力学一致的整体-表面纳维-斯托克斯-卡恩-希利亚德（bulk-surface Navier–Stokes–Cahn–Hilliard）系统，该系统描述具有移动接触线、可变接触角和传质的两相流。作者建立了非退化迁移率和奇异自由能势下全局弱解的存在性，并进一步证明了在高摩擦与解耦极限下，相应弱解（至多按子列）收敛到 Abels–Garcke–Grün 模型的弱解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两相流中的移动接触线和接触角变化是流体动力学中的经典难题，现有模型在数学分析上缺乏全局存在性结果，且不同模型之间的极限联系尚不清晰。本文旨在从数学严格性角度填补这一空白。

Method: 证明基于隐式时间离散化（implicit time-discretization）方案，并结合整体-表面自由能凸部分的次微分（subdifferential）刻画。在高摩擦与解耦极限的分析中，关键工具是整体-表面自由能凸部分的 Mosco 收敛（Mosco convergence）。

Result: 作者证明了：对于非退化迁移函数和奇异自由能势，该整体-表面两相流系统存在全局弱解；在同时取高摩擦和解耦极限时，相应弱解按子列收敛到 Abels–Garcke–Grün 模型的弱解。

Conclusion: 该工作不仅建立了所研究系统的全局弱解存在性，还通过 Mosco 收敛建立了与经典 Abels–Garcke–Grün 模型的极限一致性。文中揭示的 Mosco 收敛性质可能具有独立的研究价值。

Abstract: We study a thermodynamically consistent bulk-surface Navier--Stokes--Cahn--Hilliard system describing two-phase flows exhibiting moving contact lines, variable contact angles, and mass transfer. We establish the existence of global weak solutions for non-degenerate mobility functions and singular free-energy potentials. The proof is based on an implicit time-discretization scheme and a subdifferential characterization of the convex part of the bulk-surface free energy. Finally, we study the simultaneous high-friction and decoupling limit, and prove that corresponding weak solutions converge, up to a subsequence, to a weak solution of the Abels--Garcke--Grün model. One key ingredient of the proof is the Mosco convergence of the convex part of the bulk-surface free energy, which might be of independent interest.

</details>
