### [172] [Quantum encoding of structured light into in-plane topological spin textures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23856)
*Pavel A. Vorobyev,Daichi Kurebayashi,Oleg A. Tretiakov*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文通过微磁学模拟研究了手性铁磁薄膜中由脉冲拉盖尔-高斯光学涡旋诱导的不对称双磁子（bimeron）和反双磁子（antibimeron）成核机制，发现携带轨道角动量（OAM）|m|=1的圆偏振光可确定性产生单个拓扑磁结构，而线偏振光产生的拓扑荷直接等于OAM（Q=m）；|m|>1的光学涡旋则产生由多个自旋纹理组成的团簇，揭示了将光学量子数编码为面内拓扑磁态以实现拓扑选择性写入的途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构化光（structured light）通过多个可调光学自由度提供了控制光-物质相互作用的强大手段，但利用光学涡旋在手性磁性材料中确定性写入特定拓扑磁结构（如双磁子和反双磁子）的机制尚不明确，需要系统研究光学轨道角动量与磁手性（magnetic chirality）之间的耦合规律。

Method: 采用微磁学模拟（micromagnetic simulations）方法，分别在手性铁磁薄膜的C_nv对称性和D_2d对称性条件下，研究脉冲拉盖尔-高斯光学涡旋（pulsed Laguerre-Gaussian optical vortices）诱导不对称双磁子和反双磁子的成核过程，系统地改变OAM量子数m、光束偏振态和脉冲参数进行分析。

Result: 对于OAM |m|=1的光学涡旋：圆偏振光通过自旋角动量、轨道角动量与磁手性的协同作用确定性成核单个双磁子或反双磁子；线偏振光产生的拓扑纹理其拓扑荷直接等于OAM（Q=m）。对于OAM |m|>1的光学涡旋，会成核由多个自旋纹理组成的团簇及其他构型，其形态和拓扑荷对光学量子数和脉冲参数高度敏感。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了一条将光学量子数编码为不同的面内拓扑磁态（in-plane topological magnetic states）从而实现拓扑选择性写入（topology-selective writing）的可行路径，为光控拓扑磁存储器件的发展提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Structured light offers a powerful means of controlling light-matter interactions through multiple tunable optical degrees of freedom. Using micromagnetic simulations, we investigate the nucleation of asymmetric bimerons and antibimerons by pulsed Laguerre-Gaussian optical vortices in chiral ferromagnetic thin films with $C_{nv}$ and $D_{2d}$ symmetries, respectively. For optical vortices with orbital angular momentum (OAM) $|m|=1$, circularly polarized beams deterministically nucleate a single bimeron or antibimeron via the interplay of spin angular momentum, OAM, and magnetic chirality, whereas linearly polarized beams produce textures whose topological charge directly follows the OAM ($Q=m$). Optical vortices with OAM $|m|>1$ nucleate clusters and other configurations composed of multiple spin textures, whose morphology and topological charge depend sensitively on the optical quantum numbers and pulse parameters. These findings reveal a route to topology-selective writing through the encoding of optical quantum numbers into distinct in-plane topological magnetic states.

</details>


### [173] [Scattering-Induced Magnon Layer-Hall Transport beyond Band Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24023)
*Zhiping Xue,Zhoujian Sun,Xiyin Ye,Lei Zhang,Tao Yu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文报道了一种基于玻色子激发（磁振子，magnons）的层级霍尔效应（layer Hall effect），其起源并非几何相位机制，而是异质界面处非互易偶极散射（non-reciprocal dipolar scattering），在传统磁性异质结构中实现了显著的霍尔角（约6°），且方向可由外加磁场完全重配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层级霍尔效应此前被归因于层锁定贝里曲率（layer-locked Berry curvature），这为在传统磁性材料中实现该效应设置了根本性障碍，亟需一种不依赖拓扑工程的替代机制。

Method: 采用微观散射理论（microscopic scattering theory），在置于磁性薄膜之上的纳米线结构中，研究了纵向温度梯度驱动下的横向热霍尔电流，并系统分析了异质界面处非互易偶极散射对磁振子输运的影响。

Result: 纵向温度梯度在纳米线结构中驱动了方向相反的横向热霍尔电流，其方向可通过外加磁场完全重配置；该效应在传统磁性异质结构中产生约6°的显著霍尔角，无需拓扑工程。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了散射驱动的层级霍尔效应范式（scattering-driven paradigm），可推广至铁磁子（ferrons）和极性声子（polar phonons），并预言该霍尔响应可在传统磁性异质结构中轻易探测。

Abstract: The layer Hall effect has been exclusively attributed to layer-locked Berry curvature, posing a fundamental barrier to its realization in conventional magnets. Here we report a fundamentally distinct layer Hall effect for bosonic excitations, i.e., magnons, which originates solely from non-reciprocal dipolar scattering at heterointerfaces, thereby decoupling the phenomenon from geometric-phase mechanisms. Using a microscopic scattering theory, we demonstrate that a longitudinal temperature gradient drives opposite transverse thermal Hall currents in a nanowire atop a magnetic film, with the direction fully reconfigurable by the applied magnetic field. The effect yields a significant Hall angle of $\sim 6^{\circ}$ in conventional magnetic heterostructures, eliminating the need for topological engineering. Our findings establish a scattering-driven paradigm for layer Hall effect, extendable to ferrons and polar phonons, and predict a Hall response that is readily detectable in conventional magnetic heterostructures.

</details>


### [174] [Composite fermions in the $ν=3$ fractional quantum spin Hall effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24059)
*Hongquan Liu,D. E. Feldman*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文系统分类了MoTe₂中填充因子ν=3的分数量子自旋霍尔效应（fractional quantum spin Hall effect）下所有可能的双组分复合费米子（composite fermion）态，识别出三类已知态和两类新态，并提出了结合散粒噪声、热导和新型双量子点接触输运三种手段来甄别实验相关态的方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在GaAs和石墨烯中，分数量子霍尔态（fractional quantum Hall states）可由弱相互作用的复合费米子统一描述。作者自然期望这一统一原理同样适用于MoTe₂中提出的分数量子自旋霍尔效应，但由于量子自旋霍尔效应涉及两个自旋分量，体系需存在两种类型的复合费米子，需要对所有可能的两分量复合费米子态进行完整的分类与鉴别。

Method: 作者在填充因子ν=3下对所有两分量复合费米子态进行分类，涵盖了此前文献从不同物理视角提出的三类态以及两类新态。同时回顾了每个态的准粒子电荷、统计性质（statistics）和边界理论（edge theory），并提出了三种实验探针的组合方案：散粒噪声（shot noise）提供分数电荷信息，热导（thermal conductance）统计边界模数目，以及一种基于单边界上两个量子点接触之间输运的新探针，通过侧栅（side gate）调控边界通道形状来揭示低能边界理论的涌现对称群（emergent symmetry group）。

Result: 分类结果表明，大多数复合费米子态会打破时间反演对称性（time-reversal symmetry）。作者提出的第三种探针显示，两个量子点接触之间的电流依赖于边界通道的形状，该形状可通过侧栅控制，从而揭示低能边界理论的涌现对称群，为识别实验相关态提供了依据。

Conclusion: 通过结合三种探针，可以系统地限定并识别实验上相关的两分量复合费米子态；其中新提出的基于单边界双量子点接触输运的探针为实验鉴别提供了新颖且可行的手段。

Abstract: Well-understood fractional quantum Hall states in GaAs and graphene can be described in terms of weakly interacting composite fermions. It is natural to expect that the same unifying principle applies to the putative fractional quantum spin Hall effect in MoTe$_2$. Since the quantum spin Hall effect involves two spin components, two types of composite fermions must be present. We classify all two-component composite-fermion states at the filling factor $ν=3$. The classification includes the three classes of states, which were introduced from different physical perspectives in Refs. Sodemann Villadiego, Phys. Rev. B 110, 045114 (2024), Jian et al., Phys. Rev. X 15, 021063 (2025), and May-Mann et al., Phys. Rev. B 111, L201111, (2025), as well as two new classes of states. A majority of the composite-fermion states break the time-reversal symmetry. We review quasiparticle charges, statistics, and edge theories for each possible state. We also address a way of identifying the experimentally relevant state or states. This can be accomplished by combining three probes. First, the shot noise technique provides information about fractional charges. Second, thermal conductance helps count edge modes. The third probe is based on a new idea and involves transport between two quantum point contacts along a single edge. We find that the current from one contact to the other depends on the shape of the edge channel, which can be controlled with a side gate. The probe reveals the emergent symmetry group of the low-energy edge theory.

</details>


### [175] [Weaving Hopfions from Emergent Monopoles in a Chiral Magnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24116)
*Shoya Kasai,Kotaro Shimizu,Shun Okumura,Yukitoshi Motome*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过电流将磁性toron（三维拓扑纹理，内含涌现磁单极-反磁单极对）转化为磁hopfion（磁霍普夫子）的简单机制，并利用Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert模拟验证了其可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维磁化成像技术的进步使探索域壁和斯格明子（skyrmion）之外的拓扑自旋纹理成为可能，其中磁hopfion因其扭结拓扑有望产生非传统动力学和响应，但其受控生成仍具挑战。

Method: 基于Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert（LLG）方程进行微磁模拟，考察电流作用下磁性toron中单极-反单极对（monopole-antimonopole pair）的湮灭过程及其向hopfion的转变。

Result: 模拟显示电流可驱动单极-反单极对湮灭，将toron转化为hopfion；初始toron长度决定生成的hopfion数量，电流方向选择Hopf不变量（Hopf invariant）的符号；阈值电流对材料参数敏感，与斯格明子动力学存在密切联系。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了实验上可行的hopfion生成途径，揭示了从单极缺陷到扭结拓扑纹理的转化路径，为研究hopfion的拓扑物理提供了新方法。

Abstract: Recent advances in three-dimensional magnetization imaging techniques have opened new avenues for exploring topological spin textures beyond domain walls and skyrmions. Among them, magnetic hopfions are particularly promising, as their knotted topology is expected to give rise to unconventional dynamics and responses; however, their controlled creation remains challenging. Here we propose a simple mechanism for generating hopfions from magnetic torons, three-dimensional textures hosting an emergent monopole-antimonopole pair. Using Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert simulations, we show that an electric current drives the annihilation of this pair, converting a toron into a hopfion. The initial toron length determines the number of generated hopfions, while the current direction selects the sign of the Hopf invariant. We further find that the threshold current depends sensitively on material parameters, indicating a close connection to skyrmion dynamics. Our results establish an experimentally accessible route to hopfion creation and reveal a pathway from monopole defects to knotted topological textures.

</details>


### [176] [Electronic cooling of a TiW alloy normal-metal island using Nb-based superconducting tunnel junctions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24455)
*Joel Hätinen,Renan Pires Loreto,Arvind Kumar,Jani Taskinen,Mika Prunnila*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于钛钨（TiW）薄膜的正常金属-绝缘体-超导体（NIS）隧道结器件，证明其可实现显著电子制冷和局域电子测温，最大绝对温降为217 mK，相对温降为27%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TiW薄膜在毫开尔文温度以下仍保持非超导态，且与大规模CMOS制造工艺兼容，使其成为低温及量子隧道结器件（如NIS热传感器和电子制冷器）的理想材料，值得系统验证其制冷与测温性能。

Method: 制备TiW-Al-AlOx-Nb NIS隧道结堆叠结构，利用NIS结实现正常金属岛的电子制冷，并利用同一类型结实现局域电子测温。

Result: NIS测温计可在0.5至8.32 K范围内实现局域电子温度测量；NIS制冷在0.6至3.5 K区间内被观测到，最大绝对和相对温降分别为217 mK和27%；基于电流-电压拟合的隧道结参数计算可复现实测最佳电压标度，该标度显著低于理想低温预测值。

Conclusion: TiW基NIS隧道结器件在低温下展现出有效的电子制冷与测温能力，其实测最佳制冷电压低于理想低温理论预测，说明需要结合实际结参数而非简单理想模型来理解和优化此类器件。

Abstract: TiW thin films remain non-superconducting down to millikelvin temperatures and are compatible with large-scale CMOS manufacturing, making them attractive for cryogenic and quantum tunnel-junction devices, such as normal-metal-insulator-superconductor (NIS) thermal sensors and electron refrigerators. We demonstrate significant electronic cooling of a TiW-based normal-metal island and electron thermometry by using TiW-Al-AlOx-Nb NIS tunnel-junction stacks. The NIS thermometer enables local electron temperature measurements from 0.5 to 8.32 K. NIS cooling is observed between 0.6 and 3.5 K, with maximum absolute and relative temperature reductions of 217 mK and 27%, respectively. Calculations using tunnel-junction parameters obtained independently from current-voltage fits reproduce the measured optimum-voltage scale, which lies substantially below the ideal low-temperature prediction.

</details>


### [177] [Floquet engineering of spin-valley selective transport in jacutingaite](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24533)
*Otman Bouladiane,Kamal Azaidaoui,Clarence Cortes,David Laroze,Ahmed Jellal*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文通过散射理论研究单层jacutingaite（Pt₂HgSe₃）隧道结中的电子输运，其中仅势垒区域受到离共振圆偏振光照射。在高频极限下，光子修饰的势垒等效于具有依赖于谷的光子修饰质量项的静态Dirac哈密顿量。通过计算自旋分辨和谷分辨的透射率、反射率及Landauer电导，作者发现可实现的近完美谷过滤（|P_v|≃100%）和显著自旋极化（|P_s|∼70%），且主导极化方向可通过驱动振幅A₀、交错势V_z和衬底交换场m_s进行调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索通过光驱动势垒实现谷（valley）和自旋（spin）过滤的新机制，利用jacutingaite这一拓扑材料在光照射下的有效质量调控，为谷电子学和自旋电子学提供可控的输运调控方案。

Method: 采用高频近似（high-frequency approximation）将受光驱动的势垒约化为等效静态Dirac哈密顿量，其中光子修饰产生依赖于谷（valley-dependent）的质量项，并与交错亚晶格势V_z和衬底交换场m_s叠加提供可调质量。运用散射理论（scattering theory）计算自旋分辨和谷分辨的透射与反射系数及Landauer电导，并通过相位q_x^{ηs_z}L分析有限势垒中通道依赖的Fabry–Pérot型干涉。

Result: 光子修饰使不同谷（K与K'）的Dirac质量产生相反符号的移动，并诱导传播阈值的劈裂；有限势垒产生通道依赖的Fabry–Pérot型干涉。数值计算发现宽参数窗口内可实现近完美谷过滤（|P_v|≃100%）和显著自旋极化（|P_s|∼70%），且主导自旋和谷极化可通过调节驱动振幅A₀、V_z和m_s进行切换。

Conclusion: 光驱动的jacutingaite隧道结提供了一种灵活可控的自旋-谷滤波方案，通过驱动振幅、交错势和交换场三个参数的协同调控，可在不依赖外部磁场的情况下实现高效的自旋和谷选择输运。

Abstract: We study electron transport through a monolayer jacutingaite (Pt$_2$HgSe$_3$) tunnel junction in which only the barrier is irradiated by off-resonant circularly polarized light, while the leads remain undriven. In the high-frequency regime, the driven barrier reduces to an effective static Dirac Hamiltonian with a photon-dressed, valley-dependent mass term. A staggered sublattice potential $V_z$ and a substrate-induced exchange field $m_s$ provide additional tunable mass terms. Using scattering theory, we compute spin- and valley-resolved transmission and reflection, as well as the Landauer conductance. Photon dressing shifts the barrier {Dirac masses} with opposite signs in the ($\boldsymbol{K}, \boldsymbol{K}'$) valleys and induces a splitting of the propagation thresholds. The finite barrier then produces channel-dependent Fabry--Pérot-type interference through the phase $q_x^{ηs_z}L$. We find broad parameter windows with near-perfect valley filtering ($|P_v|\simeq 100\%$) and substantial spin polarization ($|P_s|\sim 70\%$). The dominant spin and valley polarizations can be switched by tuning the drive amplitude $A_0$, $V_z$, and~$m_s$.

</details>


### [178] [Intervalley Magnetotrions Tunable by Electric and Magnetic Fields in Buckled Two-Dimensional Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24581)
*Roman Ya. Kezerashvili,Shalva M. Tsiklauri,Anastasia Spiridonova*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文发展了褶皱二维材料（硅烯silicene、锗烯germanene、锡烯stanene）中谷间磁激子（intervalley magnetotrions）的理论框架，在垂直电场和磁场作用下，基于有效质量近似与Rytova–Keldysh势分析三粒子薛定谔方程，证明了电子与空穴有效质量相等时质心与内禀运动可严格分离，并分别以二维谐振子哈密顿量和超球谐函数方法求解，揭示了束缚能随电场磁场的单调增强行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对褶皱二维材料中多粒子（三体）激子在外场作用下的量子态与动力学尚缺乏统一理论描述的问题，本文旨在建立谷间磁激子的完整理论框架，以理解质心运动与内禀运动如何受外部电场和磁场独立调控。

Method: 在有效质量近似（effective-mass approximation）下构造三粒子薛定谔方程，采用Rytova–Keldysh相互作用势，并在高磁场区间内进行分析。对于电子与空穴有效质量相等的特殊情形，实现质心运动与内部运动的严格分离：质心运动由二维简谐振子哈密顿量描述，内部运动则通过超球谐函数方法（hyperspherical harmonics method）数值求解三体薛定谔方程。

Result: 质心运动对应量子化的朗道态（Landau states），其能量构成可电调谐的朗道面，由磁场和电场引起的有效质量变化共同控制；数值计算表明激子束缚能随磁场和电场均单调增加，归因于磁约束与电场诱导有效质量增大的协同效应；束缚能强度排序为硅烯最强，其次为锡烯和锗烯。

Conclusion: 该工作为Xene单层中磁激子的质心集体运动与内禀动力学提供了统一描述，证明两类自由度均可由外部电场和磁场独立操控，为二维材料中多粒子激子态的可调谐量子调控奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: We develop a theoretical framework for intervalley magnetotrions in buckled two-dimensional materials, including silicene, germanene, and stanene, subjected to perpendicular electric and magnetic fields. Within the effective-mass approximation, the three-particle Schrödinger equation is formulated with the Rytova--Keldysh interaction potential and analyzed in the high-magnetic-field regime. We demonstrate that intervalley trions with equal electron and hole effective masses constitute an exceptional case for which the center-of-mass and internal motions separate exactly. The center-of-mass motion is governed by a two-dimensional harmonic-oscillator Hamiltonian, leading to quantized Landau states whose energies form electrically tunable Landau surfaces controlled by the magnetic field and the electric-field dependence of the carrier effective masses. The internal motion is investigated by solving the three-body Schrödinger equation within the framework of the hyperspherical harmonics method. Numerical calculations reveal that the trion binding energy increases monotonically with both magnetic and electric fields owing to the combined effects of magnetic confinement and electric-field-induced enhancement of the effective masses. The strongest binding is obtained for silicene, followed by stanene and germanene. The present work provides a unified description of both the collective center-of-mass motion and the internal dynamics of magnetotrions in Xene monolayers, demonstrating that both degrees of freedom can be independently manipulated by external electric and magnetic fields.

</details>


### [179] [Engineering Plasmons in Oxide/Graphene Heterostructures via Interfacial Charge Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24619)
*Yuanchen Chi,Dongxu Di,Michael Fralaide,Jigang Wang,Zhe Fei*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本研究利用散射式扫描近场光学显微镜系统研究了氧化物/石墨烯异质结构中的红外表面等离激元极化子，发现超薄MoOx覆盖层通过界面电荷转移显著增强石墨烯等离激元响应，产生更长的等离激元波长、更强的条纹对比度和更低的阻尼，并可通过静电门控进一步调控，同时约3纳米厚的MoOx覆盖层能在大气环境下稳定该响应至少7个月。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 界面电荷转移为调控二维材料的光学和电子性质提供了有效途径，但此前对氧化物/石墨烯异质结构中红外等离激元的系统性调控机制仍缺乏深入理解，尤其是超薄氧化物层对电荷转移和等离激元色散的定量影响尚不明确。

Method: 采用散射式扫描近场光学显微镜（scattering-type scanning near-field optical microscopy）研究氧化物/石墨烯异质结构中的红外表面等离激元极化子；通过物理气相沉积（physical vapor deposition）制备超薄氧化物覆盖层，结合能量依赖的纳米红外成像、定量建模、厚度依赖测量和静电门控实验系统表征电荷转移掺杂行为。

Result: MoOx覆盖层显著增强石墨烯等离激元响应，产生更长等离激元波长、更强条纹对比度和降低的阻尼，而后续沉积的ZnOx覆盖层则部分逆转这些变化；厚度依赖测量显示亚纳米MoOx厚度下电荷转移掺杂快速增加，随后在更厚覆盖层下表现出较弱的远程贡献；静电门控产生与依赖门控的界面电荷重分布一致的非线性响应；约3纳米MoOx覆盖层可使等离激元响应在环境条件下稳定至少七个月。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了氧化物/石墨烯异质结构作为与可规模化制造兼容的稳健平台，为实现稳定且可调谐的红外纳米光子学和光电子器件提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Interfacial charge transfer provides an effective route for tailoring the optical and electronic properties of two-dimensional materials. Here, we investigate infrared surface plasmon polaritons in oxide/graphene heterostructures using scattering-type scanning near-field optical microscopy. Ultrathin oxide overlayers deposited by physical vapor deposition enable systematic engineering of graphene plasmons through interfacial charge redistribution. MoOx strongly enhances the plasmonic response, producing a longer plasmon wavelength, stronger fringe contrast, and reduced damping, whereas a subsequently deposited ZnOx overlayer partially reverses these changes. Energy-dependent nano-infrared imaging combined with quantitative modeling reveals an increased graphene carrier density and the resulting modification of the plasmon dispersion. Thickness-dependent measurements show a rapid increase in charge-transfer doping at sub-nanometer MoOx thicknesses, followed by a weaker long-range contribution at larger overlayer thicknesses. Electrostatic gating further modulates the carrier density and produces a nonlinear response consistent with gate-dependent interfacial charge redistribution. In addition, an approximately 3-nm-thick MoOx overlayer stabilizes the plasmonic response for at least seven months under ambient conditions. These results establish oxide/graphene heterostructures as a robust platform compatible with scalable fabrication, providing a pathway toward stable and tunable infrared nanophotonic and optoelectronic devices.

</details>


### [180] [Long-Range Order and Composite Boson Condensation in Lattice Quantum Hall States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24628)
*Fabian J. Pauw,Nathan Goldman,Felix A. Palm*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本研究运用张量网络（tensor-network）方法对具有量子霍尔态的晶格哈密顿量进行求解，首次在相互作用基态中直接揭示了复合玻色子（composite boson）凝聚及其长程关联，并证明这些关联构成可识别拓扑相变的非局域序参量（nonlocal order parameter），适用于玻色和费米模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑物质态（topological states of matter）缺乏局域序参量，尽管在连续介质中其拓扑序已被重新解释为复合玻色子的凝聚，但这一凝聚及其产生的长程关联迄今仅在最简单的试探态中得到确认，尚未在一般相互作用系统中得到直接验证。

Method: 作者将张量网络方法应用于承载量子霍尔态的晶格哈密顿量中，在相互作用基态中直接计算复合玻色子凝聚与长程关联，并检验相应的非局域序参量在平凡相与拓扑相转变处的行为，同时覆盖玻色型和费米型两种模型。

Result: 结果表明，复合玻色子的凝聚和长程关联在相互作用基态中得以直接显现，且这些关联定义的非局域序参量能够标识平凡相与拓扑相之间的相变。相应算子可通过在位分辨的局域测量在大量粒子上获取，使其可直接在量子模拟器（quantum simulators）中检验。

Conclusion: 该工作为传统固态方法难以触及的拓扑量子物质提供了新的微观研究途径，将复合玻色子凝聚的物理解释从试探态推广到一般相互作用系统。

Abstract: Topological states of matter, such as quantum Hall states, are characterized by the absence of local order parameters. In the continuum, their topological order has been reinterpreted as the condensation of nonlocal composite bosons, but this condensation and the resulting long-range correlations have so far eluded direct confirmation beyond the simplest trial states. Here, we apply tensor-network methods to lattice Hamiltonians hosting quantum Hall states, revealing this exotic order directly in interacting ground states and demonstrating both the long-range correlations and the condensation of composite bosons. These correlations define a nonlocal order parameter that identifies phase transitions between trivial and topological phases, for both bosonic and fermionic models. The corresponding operators are accessible through site-resolved, local measurements on an extensive number of particles, making them directly probable in quantum simulators. Our work opens new avenues for microscopic studies of topological quantum matter beyond the reach of traditional solid-state approaches.

</details>


### [181] [Exciton fine structure in nanocrystals: effect of cuboidal and spheroidal shapes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24663)
*M. A. Semina,O. O. Druzhinina,A. A. Golovatentko,A. V. Rodina*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出了具有椭球形和长方体形貌的纳米晶体（nanocrystals, NCs）中带边激子精细结构（band-edge exciton fine structure）的理论。通过非微扰方法处理晶体立方对称性、纳米晶体的立方形状以及单轴各向异性对空穴能态和电子-空穴交换相互作用的影响，建立了由1个空穴能级分裂常数和5个独立交换常数参数化的有效哈密顿量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纳米晶体的激子精细结构对其光学性质至关重要，但传统理论往往忽略纳米晶体形状对称性对交换相互作用的贡献。本文旨在建立更完整的理论框架，以统一描述纳米晶体中晶格对称性、形状对称性和单轴各向异性对激子精细结构的影响。

Method: 采用对称性分析（symmetry analysis）方法推导激子精细结构的有效哈密顿量，关键参数包括1个空穴能级分裂常数和5个独立的交换相互作用常数。研究以非微扰（non-perturbative）方式处理晶体立方对称性、纳米晶体立方形状和单轴各向异性三类效应，并结合数值计算分析不同几何构型下的激子能级行为。

Result: 数值计算表明，在单轴各向异性的闪锌矿（zinc-blende）纳米晶体中，空穴基态分裂的符号取决于材料参数及各向异性轴相对晶轴的取向。此外，识别出由包络波函数（envelope wavefunction）的立方对称性引起的短程交换贡献，该贡献在长方体纳米晶体中不可忽略，并导致暗激子态（dark exciton states）的显著分裂。

Conclusion: 立方形状诱导的包络波函数对称性在纳米晶体交换相互作用中具有不可忽略的贡献，特别在长方体形貌的纳米晶体中尤为显著。文章还分析了交换常数单轴各向异性对激子精细结构和振子强度（oscillator strength）的影响，并特别关注交换各向异性与较小的空穴能级各向异性分裂可比拟的情形。

Abstract: We present the theory of the band-edge exciton fine structure in nanocrystals (NCs) with spheroidal and cuboidal shapes. The effects of the cubic symmetry of the crystal lattice, the cubic shape of the NC, and NC uniaxial anisotropy on the hole energy states and electron-hole exchange interactions are considered non-perturbatively. Symmetry analysis yields an effective Hamiltonian for the exciton fine structure, parameterized by one constant for hole energy splitting and five independent constants for exchange interaction. Numerical calculations reveal that in uniaxially anisotropic zinc-blende NCs, the sign of the hole ground state splitting depends on the material parameters and on the orientation of the anisotropy axis relative to the crystallographic axes. Beyond the conventional bulk cubically-symmetric contribution to the exchange interaction, which originates from Bloch-function symmetry and is typically negligible, in nanocrystals, we identify the contribution arising from the cubic symmetry of the envelope wavefunction. This cubically symmetric envelope-induced short-range exchange is non-negligible in cuboidal NCs and induces a pronounced splitting of the dark exciton states. We further analyze the influence of uniaxial anisotropy of the exchange constants on both the exciton fine structure and the oscillator strength. Special attention is paid to NCs, where the anisotropy of the exchange constants is comparable to a relatively small hole energy anisotropic splitting.

</details>


### [182] [Visualizing flat-band spatial renormalization in rhombohedral graphene superlattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24684)
*Peng-Cheng Pan,Shihao Zhang,Yang Zhang,Ji Huang,Ling-Hui Tong,Chen-Chen Xu,Yuan Tian,Li Zhang,Lijie Zhang,Yuanyuan Hu,Wen-Xiao Wang,Zhihui Qin,Long-Jing Yin*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 利用扫描隧道显微术，直接在实空间观测到菱形堆积石墨烯/六方氮化硼（hBN）莫尔超晶格中平带（flat band）的莫尔调制空间重整化，发现该调制在莫尔周期低于约10 nm时消失——这与分数量子反常霍尔效应（FQAHE）缺失的阈值一致，揭示了莫尔周期势与涌现拓扑序之间的关键联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 菱形石墨烯/hBN莫尔超晶格展现由平带驱动的涌现相，包括超导性和分数量子反常霍尔效应，但莫尔势在微观层面的作用机制尚不明确。本文旨在通过直接实空间观测理解莫尔周期势如何调控平带及涌现相。

Method: 使用扫描隧道显微镜（STM）可视化菱形五层和四层石墨烯/hBN超晶格中莫尔调制的平带空间重整化；并结合理论建模，将观测到的机制归因于原子起伏诱导的电荷重新分布。

Result: 观测到莫尔尺度上平带的周期性能量位移，表现为空间分层的填充行为，导致相互作用区域中关联态的空间重塑；该调制在约10 nm莫尔周期以下消失，与FQAHE缺失的阈值一致。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了莫尔工程化平带重构的实空间可视化证据，解决了莫尔周期势与涌现拓扑序之间的关键联系。

Abstract: Rhombohedral graphene/hBN moiré superlattices exhibit flat-band-driven emergent phases, including superconductivity and the fractional quantum anomalous Hall effect (FQAHE), yet the microscopic role of the moiré potential remains unclear. Here, using scanning tunneling microscopy, we visualize moiré-modulated spatial renormalization of flat bands in rhombohedral pentalayer and tetralayer graphene/hBN superlattices. We observe spatially hierarchical filling, manifested as periodic energy shifts of the flat bands at the moiré scale, leading to spatial reshaping of correlated states in the interacting regime. Remarkably, this modulation vanishes below a ~10 nm moiré period--the same threshold below which the FQAHE is absent. Theoretical modeling attributes this mechanism to atomic-corrugation-induced charge redistribution. Our work provides real-space visualization of moiré-engineered flat-band reconstruction, resolving a key link between moiré periodic potential and emergent topological order.

</details>


### [183] [Hierarchical Quantum Transport from Coupled Topological Domain-Wall States in SSH Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24694)
*Alessio Palavicini,César G. Galván,Carlos Ramírez*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究了含多个拓扑畴壁（topological domain walls）的 Su-Schrieffer-Heeger（SSH）链的输运性质，发现畴壁间相互作用产生自相似的分级能谱结构。通过递归构造有效SSH结构与基于连续抽稀（decimation）的重整化描述，揭示了能谱重复碎裂的机制，表明拓扑畴壁态可作为涌现自由度用于构造多尺度输运通道和分形谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解拓扑畴壁态之间的相互作用如何在SSH链中产生超出单一畴壁的复杂能谱结构，并探索拓扑构造与自相似谱之间的关系。作者希望建立连接递归拓扑构造、有效哈密顿量（effective Hamiltonian）和一维系统中自相似谱涌现的通用框架。

Method: 作者研究含有多个拓扑畴壁的SSH链的输运性质，分析每个畴壁提供的局域态及其杂化产生的微带（miniband）。通过组合不同畴壁间距的畴壁晶格，在微带子空间中构造有效SSH结构，并将此构造递归应用于逐代生成更细的能谱结构。同时发展基于连续抽稀的有效重整化（renormalization）描述，分析有效参数的奇异点层级。

Result: 结果显示，传输谱再现了传统SSH链的特征，包括带隙形成、有限尺寸共振以及传输谱与能带结构之间的对应关系。递归构造使有效SSH谱在逐渐变窄的能量区间内反复出现，产生自相似的微带和能隙层级。重整化描述揭示了有效参数的奇异点呈现层级结构，其数量随迭代逐次增加，这些奇异点将能量轴划分为越来越精细的区间，解释了能谱的重复碎裂现象。

Conclusion: 拓扑畴壁态可作为涌现自由度，用于工程化多尺度输运通道、有效耦合和层级谱结构。该框架建立了一维系统中递归拓扑构造、有效哈密顿量与自相似谱涌现之间的普遍联系。

Abstract: We investigate the transport properties of Su-Schrieffer-Heeger (SSH) chains containing multiple topological domain walls and show that their interaction generates a hierarchy of emergent spectral structures. Each domain wall contributes a localized state inside the SSH gap, and the hybridization of these states produces minibands whose signatures are directly reflected in the transmission spectra. By combining domain-wall lattices with different domain-wall separations, we construct effective SSH structures within the miniband subspace. The resulting transmission spectra reproduce the characteristic features of conventional SSH chains, including gap formation, finite-size resonances, and the correspondence between transmission spectra and band structure. The construction can be applied recursively, generating successive generations of effective SSH structures. As a consequence, effective SSH spectra repeatedly emerge within progressively narrower energy intervals, producing a self-similar hierarchy of minibands and spectral gaps. To understand the origin of this hierarchy, we develop an effective renormalized description based on successive decimation. The effective parameters exhibit a hierarchy of interlaced singularities whose number increases at each iteration. These singularities partition the energy axis into progressively finer intervals and provide a natural interpretation of the repeated fragmentation of the spectrum. Our results show that topological domain-wall states can act as emergent degrees of freedom from which multiscale transport channels, effective couplings, and hierarchical spectral structures may be engineered. More generally, the framework introduced here establishes a connection between recursive topological constructions, effective Hamiltonians, and the emergence of self-similar spectra in one-dimensional systems.

</details>

# physics.plasm-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [184] [Physics Attention Transformer Surrogate for Rapid Vertical Instability Growth Rate Prediction: Alcator C-Mod to SPARC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24785)
*Arunav Kumar,Cesar Clauser,Theodore Golfinopoulos,Cristina Rea,Francesco Capersene,Dan Boyer,SPARC Team,Alcator C-Mod Team*

Main category: physics.plasm-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用物理注意力变换器（Physics Attention Transformer, PAT）在C-Mod和SPARC平衡构型中快速预测主导的n=0垂直不稳定性增长率及对应的二维扰动环向电流密度，在控制相关延迟下复现MEQ-FGE-L标签输出，平均绝对误差分别达5.4 s⁻¹（C-Mod）和12.7 s⁻¹（SPARC），空间特征函数误差约5%。与算子型机器学习模型（FNO2D和DeepONet）相比，PAT具有更低的归一化增长率误差和更优的空间重建性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非刚性自由边界响应模型（nonrigid free boundary response models）在控制周期（control cycle）内运行速度过慢，无法满足托卡马克等离子体实时控制的需求，因此需要开发能够快速预测主导的n=0垂直不稳定性增长率的替代方法。

Method: 采用在MEQ-FGE-L标签上训练的物理注意力变换器（Physics Attention Transformer, PAT），同时预测标量增长率以及相关的二维扰动环向电流密度分布。此外，还将其与基于算子学习的模型——二维傅里叶神经算子（FNO2D）和深度算子网络（DeepONet）进行了性能比较。

Result: 在留出的C-Mod平衡构型上平均绝对误差为5.4 s⁻¹，在合成SPARC案例上为12.7 s⁻¹，空间特征函数误差接近5%。在归一化增长率误差和空间重建方面，PAT均优于FNO2D和DeepONet。

Conclusion: PAT能够在控制相关延迟下复现MEQ-FGE-L的输出结果，可支持未来增长率裕度监测（growth rate headroom monitoring）和邻近感知形状控制（proximity aware shape control）等研究。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate rapid prediction of the dominant $n{=}0$ vertical instability growth rate in C-Mod and SPARC equilibria, where nonrigid free boundary response models are too slow for control cycle use. Using a Physics Attention Transformer trained on MEQ-FGE-L labels, we predict both the scalar growth rate and the associated two dimensional perturbed toroidal current density. We find mean absolute errors of 5.4~s$^{-1}$ on held out C-Mod equilibria and 12.7~s$^{-1}$ on synthetic SPARC cases, with spatial eigenfunction errors near 5\%. We also compared PAT with operator based ML models : FNO2D and DeepONet, where we found PAT predicts a much lower normalised growth rate error and improved spatial reconstruction. These results indicate that PAT can reproduce MEQ-FGE-L outputs at control relevant latency and could support future studies of growth rate headroom monitoring and proximity aware shape control.

</details>

### [185] [Uniform logarithmic Sobolev inequalities for the 2D Coulomb gas at the diffusive temperature scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24863)
*Matthew Rosenzweig*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该论文证明：对任意固定逆温度β>0，二维Canonical库仑气体系综在由无碰撞紧支撑光滑函数生成的闭合Dirichlet型域上满足对数索博列夫不等式（logarithmic Sobolev inequality, LSI），且常数可与N无关；β在(0,∞)的紧子集上变化时常数可均匀选取。此外还建立了加权平面高斯测度的庞加莱不等式（Poincaré inequality）与对数索博列夫不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维库仑气体（2D Coulomb gas）系综在扩散尺度（diffusive scale）下由于配对指数中N^{-1}因子，其测度性质（尤其功能不等式）尚不清楚；建立与粒子数N无关的对数索博列夫不等式是研究该系综遍历性与收敛速率的关键工具。

Method: 将方差分解为置换不变（permutation-invariant）与标签相关（label-dependent）两部分：前者用加权∂̄估计控制，后者通过相对坐标条件化与随机换位（random transpositions）处理。单点对数索博列夫不等式、条件熵不等式及排斥配分估计给出带缺陷的LSI，再结合Rothaus紧化（Rothaus tightening）融合完整的标签化庞加莱不等式与带缺陷LSI。

Result: 对每个固定β>0，二维库仑气体测度满足具有与N无关的正常数的LSI；β在(0,∞)紧子集内变化时常数可均匀选取。另对带有限个点距离正幂加权项的平面高斯测度证明了庞加莱与对数索博列夫不等式，常数仅依赖于高斯总方差与总指数。文中不声称β→0或β→∞时的一致性质。

Conclusion: 论文为扩散尺度的二维Coulomb气体系综建立了不依赖于粒子数的功能不等式族，为理解该高温/扩散尺度下系综的混合行为提供了量化工具；同时推广了加权高斯测度的谱隙与熵耗散估计，不要求均匀性在β退化极限下成立。

Abstract: For $N\ge2$ and $β>0$, consider the canonical 2D Coulomb gas ensemble \begin{equation}
  \mathrm{d}\mathbb{P}_{N,β}(Z)
  =\mathsf{Z}_{N,β}^{-1}e^{-β|Z|^2/2}
  \prod_{i<j}|z_i-z_j|^{β/N}\,\mathrm{d}Z
  \qquad\text{on }\mathbb{C}^N. \end{equation} The factor $N^{-1}$ in the pair exponent places the ensemble at the diffusive, or high-temperature, scale. We prove that, for every fixed inverse temperature $β>0$, this Gibbs measure satisfies a logarithmic Sobolev inequality (LSI) on the closed Dirichlet-form domain generated by collision-free compactly supported smooth functions, with a positive constant independent of $N$. The LSI constant may be chosen uniformly when $β$ ranges over a compact subset of $(0,\infty)$. A second main result establishes Poincaré and logarithmic Sobolev inequalities for planar Gaussian measures weighted by finite products of positive powers of distances to points, with constants depending only on the total variance of the underlying Gaussian measure and the total exponent, not on the number or locations of the points. To prove the full labeled Poincaré inequality, we decompose variance into permutation-invariant and label-dependent parts, controlled respectively by a weighted $\bar\partial$ estimate and by relative-coordinate conditioning with random transpositions; separately, one-site logarithmic Sobolev inequalities, a conditional entropy inequality, and a repulsive partition estimate give a defective logarithmic Sobolev inequality. Rothaus tightening combines the resulting full labeled Poincaré inequality with the defective logarithmic Sobolev inequality. No uniformity as $β\to0$ or $β\to\infty$ is asserted.

</details>


### [186] [Mass scaling of the near-critical Ising model in dimensions $d\geq 4$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24868)
*Romain Panis*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文研究了 d≥4 维整数格 ℤ^d 上 Ising 模型在近临界参数（β≤β_c, h≥0）下的截断两点函数衰减性质，证明其特征质量（质量即指数衰减率）满足 max((β_c−β)^{1/2}, h^{1/3})^{1+o(1)} 的标度律。证明结合了 Duminil-Copin 和 Panis 在 h=0 时的结果，并借助随机电流表示（random current representation）使用插值论证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解高维 Ising 模型在临界点附近关于温度和磁性场同时趋于临界时的关联函数衰减行为，特别是确定截断两点函数的质量（质量即衰减率）的精确标度律，这是理解临界现象普适性的核心问题之一。

Method: 本文采用两种关键技术的结合：一是利用 Duminil-Copin 和 Panis 在 h=0 情形下已建立的近临界结果；二是受到 Aizenman 和 Fernández 启发，通过模型的随机电流表示实现了插值论证，从而将 h=0 的结果推广到 h≥0 的一般情形。

Result: 在 d≥4 且 β≤β_c、h≥0 的条件下，获得了截断两点函数的近临界界，并由此得出关联质量 m(β,h) 满足 m(β,h)=max((β_c−β)^{1/2}, h^{1/3})^{1+o(1)}，其中 o(1) 在 (β,h)→(β_c,0) 时趋于 0。

Conclusion: 该结果表明在高于上临界维数（d≥4）的 Ising 模型中，温度偏离临界点与磁场偏离零点对质量具有独立且竞争的贡献，其指数分别对应热标度指数 1/2 和磁标度指数 1/3，体现了平均场（mean-field）行为的特征。

Abstract: We study the Ising model on $\mathbb{Z}^d$ with $d\geq 4$ and derive near-critical bounds on the truncated two-point function $\langleσ_0;σ_x\rangle_{β,h} := \langleσ_0σ_x\rangle_{β,h} - \langleσ_0\rangle_{β,h}\langleσ_x\rangle_{β,h}$ at parameters $β\leqβ_c$ and $h\geq 0$. As a corollary, we obtain that the associated mass (or exponential decay rate) is equal to \begin{equation*}
  \max\bigl((β_c-β)^{1/2},h^{1/3}\bigr)^{1+o(1)}, \end{equation*} where $o(1)$ tends to $0$ as $(β,h)$ tends to $(β_c,0)$. The proof combines the corresponding result at $h=0$, recently established by Duminil-Copin and Panis, with an interpolation argument inspired by Aizenman and Fernández and carried out via the random current representation of the model.

</details>
